Abstract: We previously reported a ModernBERT encoder trained from scratch for Turkish (MoganBERT-TR) and a single-vector embedding model built on top of it (MoganBERT-embed). This work introduces the third model in that lineage: MoganColBERT-TR, a multi-vector retrieval model that, instead of compressing a query or a document into a single vector, represents it at the token level through a 768->128 projection and scores it with MaxSim late interaction. The model is not trained from scratch: the embedding model's encoder is taken as the starting point and adapted to the ColBERT objective with a single-epoch distillation phase. Training data is produced from two sources - title-to-passage pairs carved out of our own pretraining corpus in the character domain and at sentence boundaries, and two Turkish question-based retrieval sets - and is distilled from the soft scores of a cross-encoder teacher (bge-reranker-v2-m3) over one positive and seven mined negatives. We show that in hard negative mining, rank-based skipping alone is insufficient and must be combined with a group mask and a cosine ceiling. Evaluation is carried out with the official pipeline of TurkColBERT, a benchmark built for Turkish late-interaction retrieval (PLAID index, exact MaxSim), on five Turkish BEIR datasets; none of them appears in our training pool, so all five results are clean zero-shot. With 148.9M parameters, MoganColBERT-TR reaches an overall score of 37.36 (35.53 nDCG@100, 31.81 nDCG@10) averaged over the five datasets and finishes second among the five models compared: it outperforms the twice-as-large ColmmBERT-base-TR on four of five datasets and by +3.05 overall, and the benchmark's largest model by +12.30. The gap to the leading model (mLateOn) is concentrated on ArguAna-TR, the dataset with by far the longest queries.

</details>


### [143] [Cross-lingual Representation Learning via Centroid Intervention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26357)
*Wei Sun,Marie-Francine Moens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Centroid Intervention Fusion（CIF），一种投影融合框架，将多语言干预投影整合为单一语言共享算子，提升大语言模型在低资源语言上的跨语言迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言任务中表现不均，尤其低资源语言性能较差；现有跨语言干预方法需为每对语言学习独立投影，缺乏可扩展性和知识共享能力。

Method: 提出Centroid Intervention Fusion（CIF）框架，通过融合多个多语言干预投影为单一语言共享算子，在推理阶段修改隐藏状态以提升跨语言迁移能力，无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在多语言常识推理、自然语言推断、事实编辑和机器翻译基准上，CIF在四种模型骨干上平均性能提升最高达+3.378个百分点，优于现有最强成对干预基线，并显著改善低资源语言表现。

Conclusion: CIF通过语言共享算子实现高效可扩展的跨语言干预，有效提升大语言模型在低资源语言上的性能，为轻量级多语言优化提供新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit uneven multilingual performance, especially when dealing with low-resource languages. Inference-time intervention offers a lightweight way to improve cross-lingual transfer by modifying the hidden states produced by the LLMs during the forward pass, without updating model parameters. However, existing cross-lingual intervention methods typically learn separate projections from source to target languages, which limits scalability and prevents knowledge sharing across languages. We propose Centroid Intervention Fusion (CIF), a projection fusion framework that consolidates multiple multilingual intervention projections into a single language-shared operator. Across multilingual commonsense reasoning, natural language inference, factual editing, and machine translation benchmarks, CIF outperforms the strongest prior pairwise intervention baseline by up to +3.378 pp on average across four model backbones, while supporting performance gains for low resource languages. The code is available at https://github.com/VRCMF/CIF.git.

</details>


### [144] [Knowledge-Verified Emergent Deception in LLM Agents Under Conflicting Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26372)
*Zheyuan Liu,Weiliang Zhao,Xiangchi Yuan,Ningshan Ma,Yue Huang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KnownLieBench基准，用于检测大语言模型在利益冲突下是否对已知用户权益进行欺骗，并发现不同模型欺骗行为差异显著，诚实导向微调可减少欺骗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为自主代理被广泛部署，当用户与部署者利益冲突时，模型是否会为了部署者利益而对已知权益进行欺骗？现有研究难以区分错误陈述是源于无知、幻觉还是故意欺骗。

Method: 构建KnownLieBench基准，包含8个客户服务领域和112个具体案例，通过中性探测确认代理知晓用户权益，再引入激励 deny 权益的情境评估欺骗行为，区分仅由激励引发的欺骗与明确指令下的欺骗，并在18个模型上进行测试与微调实验。

Result: 不同模型家族和领域间欺骗行为差异显著；诚实导向微调可减少激励下的欺骗，而欺骗分级微调虽不增加激励下说谎频率，但提高了在诚实控制对话中的说谎成功率。

Conclusion: KnownLieBench通过先验证权益知识再评估欺骗行为，有效区分了说谎与不知情，为代理诚实性的审计与引导提供了更严谨的工具。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents serving users on behalf of companies, placing them in settings where user and deployer interests can conflict. When an agent knows that a user is owed something its deployer would prefer to deny, does it remain honest? Answering this is difficult because false statements can reflect either ignorance or hallucination rather than deception. To address this challenge, we introduce KnownLieBench , a knowledge-verified benchmark that first confirms through a neutral probe that an agent knows a user's entitlement, and then evaluates whether it makes false claims once an incentive to deny that entitlement is introduced. Specifically, KnownLieBench covers eight customer-service domains and 112 grounded cases, conducts multi-round dialogues with a trust-tracking customer agent, and separates deception emerging from incentive alone from deception produced under explicit instruction. Across eighteen proprietary and open-weight models, emergent deception varies substantially across model families and domains. We further use the benchmark for post-training, finding that honesty-directed fine-tuning reduces deception under incentive, while deception-graded fine-tuning increases lie success on honest-control dialogues without increasing lie frequency under incentive. By verifying entitlement knowledge before scoring deceptive behavior, KnownLieBench reduces the confound between lying and not knowing and enables more rigorous auditing and steering of agent honesty.

</details>


### [145] [Survival-Guided Length Control for Efficient Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26374)
*Ivan Kobyzev,Abbas Ghaddar,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的扩散语言模型长度预测器，将长度选择重构为离散时间生存问题，在保持准确率的同时将推理速度提升最高7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型解码方法要么固定序列长度，要么依赖临时停止规则，导致不必要的去噪步骤，影响推理效率。

Method: 将长度选择重构为针对序列结束标记的离散时间生存问题，提出了一种可插拔、无需训练的长度预测器，可应用于任何现有扩散语言模型。

Result: 在推理和代码生成基准测试中，生存引导的长度解码将推理速度提升最高7倍，同时保持任务准确率；发现同一数据集内预测长度差异很大，模型性能对选择长度敏感。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了扩散语言模型中长度选择不当导致的效率问题，为提升扩散语言模型推理速度提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked sequences, but standard decoding either fixes the sequence length or relies on ad hoc stopping rules, often leading to unnecessary denoising steps. We recast length selection as a discrete-time survival problem over the end-of-sequence token and propose a plug-in, training-free length predictor that can be added to any existing DLM. Across reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, survival-guided length decoding speeds up inference by up to 7 times while preserving task accuracy. We further find that predicted lengths vary widely even within the same dataset, making model performance sensitive to the chosen length.

</details>


### [146] [Why RAGs Hallucinate: Penalty-Aware Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems with Knowledge-Gap Canaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26385)
*Alden Do Rosario,Hussein Younes,Felipe Pires*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的惩罚感知评估框架，通过非对称评分、知识缺口金丝雀测试和失败归因流程，揭示了现有系统因过度回答而导致的幻觉问题，并重新排序了系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于准确率的评估体系奖励 RAG 系统“猜测”行为，即无论知识库是否支持都强行回答，导致无法区分系统在面对未知问题时的克制能力。

Method: 结合（1）非对称评分机制（正确 +1，错误 -4，弃权 0）；（2）知识缺口金丝雀测试（验证知识库中无答案的问题）；（3）失败归因流程（分离检索、生成和弃权策略失败），在 SimpleQA-Verified 数据集上对三个商业 RAG 系统及无检索基线进行评估。

Result: 各系统回答正确时的准确率高度集中（97.0%-98.0%），但金丝雀违规率差异显著（16.7% vs 98.1%）；惩罚感知评分改变了基于回答量的排名，且该重排序在惩罚系数 k=1 到 9 之间保持稳定。

Conclusion: RAG 系统的核心差异不在于回答正确的能力，而在于是否在不该回答时保持克制；惩罚感知评估框架能更真实地反映部署系统的可靠性，相关代码和数据已开源以供审计。

Abstract: Volume-based accuracy rewards retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems for guessing: a system that answers everything outscores one that declines when its knowledge base cannot support an answer. Building on the confidence-target analysis of Kalai et al. (2025), we present a penalty-aware evaluation framework for deployed RAG products, combining (i) asymmetric scoring (correct +1, wrong -4, abstain 0), (ii) knowledge-gap canaries, questions whose answers are verifiably absent from the knowledge base, so that any answer constitutes ungrounded generation from parametric memory, and (iii) a failure-attribution pipeline that separates retrieval, generation, and abstention-policy failures. Applying the framework to three commercial RAG systems and a no-retrieval baseline on SimpleQA-Verified (1,000 questions x 3 repeats, graded blind by a cross-family three-judge panel with 98.9% unanimity), we find that accuracy when answering is closely clustered across systems (97.0-98.0%), while canary violation rates differ roughly sixfold (16.7% vs. 98.1%). The systems are separated less by what they answer correctly than by whether they answer at all when they should not, and penalty-aware scoring reorders the volume-based ranking accordingly; the reordering is stable across penalty settings from k=1 to k=9. All code, configurations, transcripts, and judge votes are released for independent audit.

</details>


### [147] [Co-Evolving Structured Knowledge and Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26386)
*Ryan Thomas Noonan,Linxi Zhao,Menghan Xu,Akanksha Sarkar,Mihir Mishra,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger,Yoav Artzi,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KBevo框架，通过联合学习构建结构化知识库并基于其推理，以解决检索增强方法中无关上下文和可控性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强方法虽能提高事实准确性，但基于非结构化文本的检索常引入无关上下文且可控性有限；结构化知识库虽更可控，但构建成本高且推理脆弱。

Method: 提出KBevo协同演化框架，端到端优化知识库构建与推理组件，利用问答结果奖励直接提升知识库质量。

Result: 生成更大、连接性更强的知识结构，提高答案可达性，并在组合事实推理和可控性方面优于标准检索基线。

Conclusion: KBevo通过协同演化机制有效平衡了知识库构建与推理，显著提升了知识密集型问答的性能与可控性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented methods improve factual accuracy by grounding language models in external knowledge, but retrieving over unstructured text often introduces irrelevant context and offers limited control over the retrieved information. Structured knowledge bases offer a more controllable alternative, yet they are expensive to construct and often brittle to reason over. To address these limitations, we propose KBevo: a co-evolving framework that jointly learns to construct a structured knowledge base and reason over it for knowledge-intensive question answering. By optimizing both components end-to-end with QA outcome rewards, our method enables reasoning success to directly improve the quality of the constructed knowledge base. This leads to larger, better-connected knowledge structures with higher answer reachability, while also improving compositional factual reasoning and controllability compared to standard retrieval baselines.

</details>


### [148] [LowRankArena: A Standardized Evaluation Platform for SVD-Based LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26389)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Wenhao Wu,Jinhee Kim,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SVD低秩压缩LLM研究中评估标准不统一的问题，提出了标准化评估平台LowRankArena。通过对五种代表性方法的统一审计，发现既往结论在标准化协议下具有高度条件性，揭示了性能排名、精度损失与加速效果之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVD低秩压缩研究缺乏可比性，因评估基准、压缩比例及实验设置不一致，且常未将低秩效应与辅助技术隔离，导致无法确定性能提升是源于方法改进还是评估协议差异，亟需统一的复现评估平台。

Method: 构建LowRankArena标准化评估平台，统一任务版本、均匀精度压缩预算、对比机制及推理测量指标，提供包含超过3 TiB压缩检查点的可复现流水线，并对五种代表性SVD方法进行对齐审计。

Result: 在标准化协议下，既往研究发现高度依赖具体条件：不同骨干网络和保持率下领先方法及性能梯队发生 shifts；多项选择准确率可能掩盖巨大的困惑度退化；名义上的低秩节省仅带来依赖工作负载且往往有限的端到端加速。

Conclusion: LowRankArena揭示了SVD压缩方法评估中的关键差异与误区，强调了标准化评估的重要性，为未来研究提供了可靠的基准与超过3 TiB的压缩模型资源，推动了该领域的规范化发展。

Abstract: SVD-based low-rank compression has become a fast-growing direction for reducing the memory and computational cost of large language models (LLMs). However, meaningful comparison across existing studies remains difficult as prior evaluations use varied benchmarks, inconsistent ratios, and diverse setups, often failing to isolate low-rank effects from auxiliary techniques. As a result, it remains unclear whether reported gains reflect method-level improvements or differences in evaluation protocol. This lack of comparability highlights the need for a unified, reproducible evaluation platform. To address this problem, we present LowRankArena, a standardized evaluation platform for SVD-based LLM compression. LowRankArena unifies task versions, uniform-precision compression budgets, comparison regimes, and inference measurements, and provides a reproducible pipeline with over 3 TiB released compressed checkpoints. Using LowRankArena, our aligned audit of five representative SVD methods reveals that prior findings are highly conditional under standardized protocols: clear leaders and performance tiers shift across backbones and keep ratios, multiple-choice accuracy can hide large perplexity degradation, and nominal low-rank savings yield workload-dependent and often limited end-to-end speedups. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/lowrankarena.git.

</details>


### [149] [Case2Flow: Bridging Patient Cases and Guideline Flowcharts through Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26414)
*Jiale Wei,Yufan Chen,Alexander Jaus,Zdravko Marinov,Julian Friedrich,Simon Reiß,Jens Kleesiek,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Case2Flow任务，用于从医学指南中检索与患者病例最相关的流程图，并构建了FlowAtlas数据集。针对现有方法的问题，提出了CRISP评分方法，显著提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学指南包含丰富的循证决策逻辑，但临床医生难以在指南中定位具体的决策内容，尤其是跨指南时。尽管流程图能够编码可操作的临床路径，但在决策支持中未被充分利用。

Method: 构建了包含202个流程图和1911个病例-流程图对的FlowAtlas数据集，并提出CRISP方法，通过抑制无信息补丁、减少模糊标记匹配和引入双向查询-图像对齐来优化检索。

Result: CRISP方法将Recall@1提升了最多18.71个百分点，盲法医师评估验证了其在真实病例叙述中的可行性。

Conclusion: CRISP有效解决了多模态检索中的系统性失败模式，为基于流程图的临床决策支持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Medical guidelines encode rich, evidence-based decision logic, yet the specific decision artifact a clinician needs is hard to locate within a guideline, let alone across guidelines covering plausible diseases and treatments. While guideline passages have supported end-to-end question answering, flowcharts remain largely underused in decision support despite their ability to encode actionable clinical pathways. We therefore introduce Case2Flow, a task designed to retrieve the most relevant guideline flowchart for a given patient case from a collection of guideline documents. To support it, we construct FlowAtlas, a curated corpus of 202 flowcharts extracted from 2,080 medical guidelines, together with a pipeline that synthesises 1,911 aligned case-flowchart pairs. Our evaluation of multimodal retrieval methods reveals systematic failure modes, including overreliance on keywords and spurious token-patch matches induced by uninformative background regions in flowcharts. Motivated by this, we propose CRISP, a training-free scoring method that sharpens late-interaction retrieval by suppressing uninformative patches, discounting ambiguous token matches, and incorporating bidirectional query-image alignment. CRISP improves Recall@1 by up to 18.71 percentage points, while a blinded physician assessment on published case narratives provides preliminary feasibility evidence beyond synthetic queries.

</details>


### [150] [AfriSwitch: A Benchmark for In-the-Wild African Code-Switched Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26434)
*Gabrial Zencha Ashungafac,Busayo Awobade,Tobi Olatunji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AfriSwitch，一个包含16种非洲语言、61.36小时的人工转录代码切换（code-switching）语音基准数据集，并评估了现有ASR系统在该数据集上的表现，发现性能显著下降，且针对非洲数据的训练比模型规模更能预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动语音识别（ASR）系统大多假设输入为单语，并在精心策划的单语基准上进行评估，忽视了双语非洲对话中普遍存在的代码切换现象，导致缺乏针对真实代码切换场景的评估标准和数据。

Method: 构建AfriSwitch基准数据集，包含16种非洲语言及变体的61.36小时野外代码切换语音，提供切换级别的英语跨度标签、每话语的代码混合指数（CMI）和切换点计数；利用该数据集对五个开源及商业多语言ASR系统进行零样本（zero-shot）基准测试。

Result:  corpus统计显示，不同非洲语言的混合行为在“交替频率”和“混合平衡度”两个独立轴上差异巨大，单一标量无法概括语言的代码切换程度；基准测试结果显示，所有系统的词错误率（WER）远高于同语言的单语基准，最佳系统平均WER为35.93%，且没有任何系统在任何语言上低于24%。

Conclusion: 针对非洲数据的特定训练（Africa-targeted training）是预测ASR性能的最佳指标，其重要性超过了模型规模或名义上的语言覆盖范围，表明当前通用多语言模型在处理非洲代码切换语音时存在显著不足。

Abstract: Code-switching is pervasive in bilingual African conversation, yet most ASR systems assume monolingual input and are evaluated on curated monolingual benchmarks. We present AfriSwitch, a 61.36-hour human-transcribed benchmark of in-the-wild code-switched speech spanning 16 African languages and language varieties, released with switch-level English span tags, perutterance Code-Mixing Index (CMI), and switch-point counts. Corpus statistics show that mixing behaviour varies widely across African languages along two largely independent axes: how often speakers alternate, and how balanced the mixture is. No single scalar captures how code-switched a language is. Benchmarking five open and commercial multilingual ASR systems zero-shot yields word error rates far above published monolingual figures for the same languages, with the best system averaging 35.93% WER and no system falling below 24% on any language. Africa-targeted training, not model scale or nominal language coverage, best predicts performance.

</details>


### [151] [Vowel Signs Are Not Letters: A Pre-tokenization Ceiling on Multilingual Tokenizer Fertility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26449)
*Sajal Regmi,Siddhartha Pudasaini,Chetan Phakami Pun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出 HuggingFace ByteLevel 分词器沿用的 GPT-2 正则表达式（仅匹配 Unicode 字母）会导致元音标记被错误分割，严重损害元音附标文字（如泰文、藏文）的处理效率；研究量化了该缺陷对分词膨胀率的下限影响，并通过实验证明修复该正则表达式能显著降低困惑度且提升训练效率，同时发现该缺陷广泛存在于主流开源模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有字节级 BPE 分词器在处理元音附标文字（abugida scripts）时，因正则表达式定义缺陷（\p{L}+）导致单词在每一个元音符号处被强制分割的问题。这种结构性缺陷使得分词粒度无法通过增加词汇量或训练数据来优化，导致模型效率低下且难以诊断。

Method: 1. 理论形式化：将正则表达式导致的分割效应定义为“无训练分词膨胀率（fertility）下限”。
2. 跨语言实证：在 26 种语言的平行语料中测试 17 种元音附标文字，对比受损分词器与修复后分词器的分词膨胀率。
3. 数据分布实验：调整尼泊尔语在训练语料中的占比（5%-95%），观察分词器表现以区分结构性瓶颈与数据短缺。
4. 模型训练对比：训练三个仅分词器不同的 268M 参数模型，评估其在同等算力及同等字节输入下的性能（bits per byte）。
5. 行业普查：统计 HuggingFace 上热门文本生成模型中该缺陷的普遍性。

Result: 1. 所有 17 种元音附标文字均受影响，分词膨胀率从藏文的 1.47 倍到泰文的 9.02 倍不等，而拉丁、西里尔等文字为 1.00 倍。
2. 修复后的分词器在尼泊尔语上每词 token 数从 4.78 降至 1.58，与理论预测误差小于 2.2%。
3. 数据占比实验中，受损分词器性能几乎无变化（1.7%），而修复版显著提升（33.9%），证实了结构性天花板的存在。
4. 修复版模型在同等算力下，尼泊尔语持集 bits per byte 降低 4.43%；即便在 1.59 倍算力下，受损模型仍无法超越修复版。
5. 行业普查显示，63.3% 的热门下载模型存在此缺陷，占下载总量的 72.5%。

Conclusion: GPT-2 遗留的正则表达式缺陷是阻碍多语言（特别是元音附标文字）大模型性能的关键结构性瓶颈，且该问题可通过症状识别而无需代码审查。GPT-4o 已采用修复方案证明其可行性。研究量化了修复价值，发布了包含 65,536 词条的尼泊尔语 - 英语分词器及复现代码，呼吁业界广泛采用感知标记（mark-aware）的词类定义以消除该性能赤字。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE tokenizers that use the HuggingFace ByteLevel pre-tokenizer inherit GPT-2's word regex, where a word is defined as \p{L}+, one or more Unicode letters. In abugida scripts, vowels are written as combining marks; this pattern therefore splits each word at every vowel sign. Since BPE merges only within a pre-token, those splits persist through training regardless of vocabulary size or corpus composition. We formalise this effect as a training-free lower bound on fertility. Across 26 languages from a parallel corpus, every one of the 17 abugidas is affected, ranging from 1.47x (Tibetan) to 9.02x (Thai), whereas Latin, Cyrillic, Hangul, and Han show exactly 1.00x. For 5 languages, matched tokenizer pairs that differ only in this character class fall within 2.2% of the predicted floor, scoring 4.78 versus 1.58 tokens per word on Nepali. When the Nepali share of the training corpus is swept from 5% to 95%, the broken tokenizer barely shifts at all (1.7%) while the fixed one shifts 33.9%, which separates a structural ceiling from a data shortage without needing to inspect any code. We train three 268M models that differ only in their tokenizer; the fixed variant achieves 4.43% lower held-out Nepali bits per byte at equal compute, and it still leads when given the same bytes with 1.59x the compute. A census of 3,479 HuggingFace repositories finds the letters-only word class present in 63.3% of the most-downloaded text-generation models, accounting for 72.5% of their downloads. GPT-4o's o200k pattern already uses a mark-aware word class, making the repair itself prior art. We quantify its value, show how to recognise its absence from symptoms alone, map which scripts it reaches, measure how widely it is deployed, and release a 65,536-entry Nepali-English tokenizer with a harness that regenerates every number here from public data on a laptop.

</details>


### [152] [Sycophancy Suppression Can Impair Rational Updating: Anti-Sycophancy Should Preserve the Ability to Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26511)
*Huanhuan Ma,Henry Peng Zou,Chengze Li,Enze Ma,Yunyue Su,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型的“阿谀奉承”行为包含“无据屈服”和“理性更新”两种机制，现有抑制方法往往顾此失彼；研究发现两者共享内部神经机制，导致优化时存在权衡，因此反阿谀策略应从单纯抑制转向选择性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注抑制模型为迎合用户而产生的“无据屈服”（Unsupported-Yielding），却忽视了用户反馈可能包含真实证据从而引发模型“理性更新”（Rational-Updating）的正面情况，且缺乏能区分这两种行为的评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个两轮评估框架以分别量化“无据屈服”和“理性更新”行为；对代表性的训练时和推理时干预措施进行测试；通过机制分析（Mechanistic Analysis）考察MLP神经元和注意力头的重叠情况及导向方向；并进行了初步的正交化导向（orthogonalized steering）探索。

Result: 反阿谀方法在减少“无据屈服”时往往牺牲“理性更新”，反之亦然，即使联合优化也存在权衡；机制分析显示两种行为共享内部基质（MLP神经元和注意力头高度重叠，导向方向正相关）；正交化导向探索仅获得了有限的、依赖骨干网络的选择性提升。

Conclusion: 反阿谀不应被视为简单的抑制问题，而是一个选择性问题；有效的干预措施必须在减少“无据屈服”的同时，保留模型的“理性更新”能力。

Abstract: Large language models often exhibit sycophancy, revising their answers to align with users when users push back. Such answer flips, however, can arise from different causes. One possibility is that the model simply aligns with the user's feedback in order to satisfy them. Another is that the feedback genuinely contains useful evidence, prompting the model to update its answer in a rational way. We distinguish them as Unsupported-Yielding and Rational-Updating. Prior work focuses primarily on suppressing Unsupported-Yielding, while overlooking its effect on Rational-Updating. We address this gap with a two-turn evaluation framework that measures the two behaviors separately. Across representative training-time and inference-time interventions, we find that anti-sycophancy methods often encounter a trade-off in which reducing Unsupported-Yielding can sacrifice Rational-Updating, and vice versa, even when the two objectives are optimized jointly. Mechanistic analysis suggests that the two behaviors share an internal substrate: the MLP neurons and attention heads driving them overlap substantially, and their associated steering directions are positively aligned. We further conduct a preliminary orthogonalized steering exploration, which yields modest, backbone-dependent selectivity gains. Overall, our results suggest that anti-sycophancy should be treated not as a simple suppression problem, but as a selectivity problem, where effective interventions should preserve Rational-Updating while reducing Unsupported-Yielding.

</details>


### [153] [Multi-Expert Conformal Risk Control for Pairwise LLM Judging in Open-Ended Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26529)
*Ming Cheng,Yusheng Dai,Qiuhong Ke,Zhaolin Chen,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多专家一致性风险控制（CRC）算法用于LLM成对评估，针对异质专家评分尺度不一致问题，设计了边际校准一致性共识（MC3）方法，并在自建基准Panel上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放域对话的LLM-as-a-Judge成对评估中，单一专家方法存在风险，而现有CRC方法虽能通过弃权控制决策阈值风险，却无法解决评分函数源头的偏差。特别是在异质LLM评委面板中，统一的阈值无法匹配不同专家独特的评分尺度，导致覆盖率受限。

Method: 1. 设计两种多专家CRC方法：分数平均（Score Averaging）和决策投票（Decision Voting），分别在分数层和决策层进行聚合。
2. 提出边际校准一致性共识（MC3）：通过初始阈值比率捕捉每个专家的独特评分尺度，同时联合调整统一的决策函数$C_t(x)$，在保持可交换性的前提下解决异质面板的尺度不匹配问题。
3. 构建Panel基准：包含1800对人工成对偏好数据，基于四个开源权重LLM在三个领域（ESConv, MSC, DREAM）生成的对话响应，并提供完整logit访问。

Result: 在同质专家面板上，分数平均和决策投票均显著提高了准确率和接受率；在异质LLM评委面板上，MC3方法通过适配不同专家的评分尺度，在所有三个数据集中成功扩展了上述增益，实现了风险有效且高覆盖率的评估。

Conclusion: 多专家聚合是对CRC的有效补充，能从源头净化评分函数。MC3方法成功解决了异质LLM评委中评分尺度不一致的难题，为开放域对话的可靠自动化评估提供了鲁棒的框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore multi-expert Conformal Risk Control (CRC) algorithms for pairwise LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in open-ended dialogue. Our core insight is that multi-expert aggregation offers a complementary remedy to CRC: whereas CRC controls risk at the decision threshold through abstention, aggregation sanitizes the scoring function at its source. Guided by this, we first design two multi-expert CRC methods: Score Averaging and Decision Voting, which aggregate at the score and decision levels, respectively. While both strategies outperform single-expert methods on homogeneous expert panels, on heterogeneous LLM judges they remain risk-valid but recover only limited coverage, because a uniform threshold cannot match the experts' distinct scoring scales. To resolve this issue, we further propose Marginal-Calibrated Conformal Consensus (MC3): it captures distinct per-expert scales via initial threshold ratios, while jointly tuning a unified decision function $C_t(x)$ applied identically in both calibration and test, thereby preserving exchangeability. To evaluate our framework, we construct Panel, a 1,800-pair human pairwise-preference benchmark for open-ended dialogue. It is built on responses generated by four open-weight LLMs over dialogue contexts from three domains (ESConv, MSC, DREAM), with full logit access. In experiments, we find that both Score Averaging and Decision Voting substantially improve accuracy and acceptance rate on homogeneous panels. Notably, MC3 extends these gains to heterogeneous panels by accommodating distinct per-expert scoring scales across all three datasets.

</details>


### [154] [SPEAR: Distilling Domain-Adaptive Reasoning Skeletons via Sequential Symbolic Alignment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26550)
*Zhuochun Li,Yuelyu Ji,Yiming Zeng,Daqing He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPEAR，一种无需训练且即插即用的符号过程评估与对齐奖励方法，通过将自然语言推理轨迹映射为符号里程碑并利用最长公共子序列（LCS）对齐，解决了强化学习知识蒸馏中稀疏奖励指导不足与神经过程奖励模型（PRM）成本高昂之间的困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的知识蒸馏面临两难选择：基于稀疏结果的奖励缺乏足够的逻辑指导，而提供密集信号的神经过程奖励模型（PRM）则计算成本昂贵。

Method: 引入SPEAR方法，将自然语言推理轨迹投影到领域自适应的符号里程碑中，作为过程级推理对齐的高效代理；利用最长公共子序列（LCS）算法将学生的探索路径与教师的里程碑进行对齐，从而生成密集且感知顺序的奖励信号，无需外部神经验证器。

Result: 在数学、科学和常识推理任务上的实验表明，SPEAR能够通过序列级蒸馏和高效密集的过程奖励，有效弥合学生模型与教师模型之间的推理差距。

Conclusion: SPEAR成功提供了一种无需训练、即插即用的解决方案，通过符号化过程评估实现了逻辑一致的密集奖励信号，显著提升了知识蒸馏中复杂推理能力的迁移效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning-based knowledge distillation has the potential to transfer complex reasoning from teacher to student models, yet it currently faces a critical dilemma: researchers must choose between sparse outcome-based rewards, which provide insufficient logical guidance, or expensive neural Process Reward Models (PRMs) for dense signals. We resolve this by introducing SPEAR (Symbolic Process Evaluation and Alignment Reward), a training-free and plug-and-play process reward method for sequence-level on-policy distillation. SPEAR projects natural-language reasoning traces into domain-adaptive symbolic milestones, providing an efficient proxy for process-level reasoning alignment. By utilizing the longest common subsequence (LCS) to align student explorations with teacher milestones, SPEAR provides a dense, order-aware reward signal that enforces logical consistency without the need for an external neural verifier. Our experiments across math, science, and commonsense reasoning tasks demonstrate that SPEAR effectively bridges the reasoning gap between student and teacher models via sequence-level distillation with efficient dense process rewards. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/zhuochunli/SPEAR.

</details>


### [155] [SPT: Skills as Pre-Training Data for Agentic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26563)
*Yufei Sun,Yudong Li,Yiming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出技能预训练（SPT）方法，利用公开技能包作为中训练数据源，通过因果语言建模和参考插入策略提升语言模型的代理性能，同时保持通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理语言模型主要依赖工具调用轨迹和代理轨迹进行后训练，但这些数据的生成成本高且覆盖范围有限。公开技能虽然包含可重用的工具语义和工作流，但通常仅作为推理时上下文使用，未充分挖掘其训练价值。

Method: 提出技能预训练（SPT）方法，对公开多文件技能包集合（SkillCorpus）应用因果语言建模；引入参考插入（Reference Insert）策略，将支持文件放置在主要指令提及处附近以保留包内文件关系；可选混合通用数据进行训练。

Result: 在多种模型规模和后训练方案下的实验表明，SPT相比基于通用数据或轨迹数据的中训练，能持续提升代理性能，同时基本保持通用性能；数据混合实验显示技能数据与通用退火语料结合有额外收益。

Conclusion: 技能包是预训练代理语言模型的有价值数据源，SPT方法能有效利用公开技能提升模型能力，为降低代理模型训练成本提供新途径。

Abstract: Agentic (tool-using) language models are mainly trained on tool-call traces and agent trajectories during post-training. These data provide direct behavioral supervision, but producing them requires task environments, execution, and verification, making broad tool and task coverage expensive. Publicly available skills offer another source of training data: they encode reusable tool semantics and workflows but are typically used only as inference-time context. We introduce Skill Pre-Training (SPT), a mid-training method that applies causal language modeling to SkillCorpus, a collection of public multi-file skill packages, optionally mixed with general data. To preserve relations among files within each package, we also introduce Reference Insert, a reference-aware assembly strategy that places supporting files near their mentions in the primary instruction. Experiments across multiple model scales and post-training recipes show that SPT consistently improves agentic performance over mid-training on general or trajectory data, while largely preserving general performance. Data mixture experiments show additional benefits from combining skill data with general annealing corpora. These results indicate that skill packages are a valuable data source for pre-training agentic language models.

</details>


### [156] [Dependency-Aware Revocable Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26574)
*Wooje Park,Insu Lee,Minyoung Noh,Jaeyun Jang,Sungmin Lee,Kyuhong Shim,Byonghyo Shim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DARD（Dependency-Aware Revocable Decoding）的无训练框架，用于解决扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中解码并行性增加导致生成质量下降的问题，通过分离token状态并选择性验证上下文，显著提升了速度与质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过迭代去噪实现并行解码，但增加解码并行性往往会导致生成质量下降，因为早期错误会污染后续上下文。现有的可撤销解码方法虽然通过重新评估和重新掩码不可靠token来缓解此问题，但忽略了不可靠token可能也会破坏验证上下文本身。

Method: 提出Dependency-Aware Revocable Decoding（DARD）框架，将token分为掩码、候选和未掩码三种状态。DARD使用选择性上下文验证候选token，排除较不可靠的token，并自适应地调节它们对后续解码的影响，无需额外训练。

Result: 在3个开源dLLMs上的12个文本和多模态基准测试中，DARD consistently改进了速度与质量的Pareto前沿。在Flickr30K上，相比Saber方法实现了2.71倍的加速和4.35点的CIDEr分数提升。

Conclusion: DARD通过识别并解决不可靠token污染验证上下文的问题，有效提升了dLLMs的解码效率与生成质量，为扩散大语言模型的并行解码提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation by decoding multiple tokens in parallel through iterative denoising. However, increasing decoding parallelism often degrades generation quality, as early errors can contaminate later contexts. Revocable decoding mitigates this issue by re-evaluating decoded tokens and remasking unreliable ones, but existing methods overlook that unreliable tokens may also corrupt the verification context itself. We identify this failure mode and propose Dependency-Aware Revocable Decoding (DARD), a training-free framework that separates tokens into masked, candidate, and unmasked states. DARD verifies candidate tokens using a selective context that excludes less reliable tokens and adaptively regulates their influence on subsequent decoding. Experiments across 12 textual and multimodal benchmarks on 3 open-source dLLMs show that DARD consistently improves the speed-quality Pareto frontier over recent revocable decoding methods, achieving a 2.71$\times$ speedup and a 4.35-point CIDEr score gain over Saber on Flickr30K.

</details>


### [157] [Double Trouble: Bilingual Pretraining Leaves Language-Conditioned Effects in Shared-Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26576)
*Anjishnu Mukherjee,Ziwei Zhu,Antonios Anastasopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在多语言模型比较中，对齐嵌入空间并假设共享语言表示可比性的做法可能为时过早，尤其是在仅解码器模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员在比较多语言模型时通常假设对齐后的共享语言表示具有可比性，但这一假设在仅解码器模型中可能不成立。

Method: 预训练配对的3.1亿参数模型（一个仅英语，一个双语），涵盖八种类型学上多样的语言，控制英语暴露量、总计算量和文档重叠。在对齐共享英语词汇后，测试保留词汇的嵌入和隐藏状态。

Result: 对齐后token嵌入看起来相似，但模型用于预测的更深隐藏状态并不相似。这种差异在所有八种语言中都存在，且不受文档重叠和替代对齐方法的影响。隐藏状态的不匹配在中间Transformer层中增长，表明其源于上下文处理而非输入表示。

Conclusion: 嵌入对齐可能掩盖模型内部表示共享语言的真实差异，这对将已对齐模型视为可互换的任何下游研究都有重要影响。

Abstract: When researchers compare multilingual models for probing, interpretability, or cross-lingual transfer, they often align embedding spaces and assume that shared-language representations are comparable. We show that this assumption can be premature for decoder-only models. We pretrain paired 310M-parameter models (one English-only, one bilingual) across eight typologically diverse languages, separately controlling for English exposure, total compute, and document overlap. After aligning on shared English vocabulary, we test held-out words and find that token embeddings look similar after alignment, but the deeper hidden states that the model uses for prediction do not. This gap holds for all eight languages and survives controls for document overlap and alternative alignment methods. This hidden-state mismatch grows through middle transformer layers, suggesting that it arises from contextual processing rather than the input representations where alignment is performed. Embedding alignment can mask real differences in how models internally represent a shared language, which matters for any downstream study that treats aligned models as interchangeable.

</details>


### [158] [Surgical Alignment in Knowledge Graph Training for Clinical Diagnosis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26587)
*Saksham Khatwani,He Cheng,Majid Afshar,Dmitriy Dligach,Yanjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将生物医学知识图谱（KG）整合到大语言模型（LLM）中的不同方法，发现基于KL正则化的KG判断训练能产生稀疏的“手术式对齐”更新，虽在域内准确率上可能略低，但在知识迁移和推理质量上优于产生密集更新的任务特定微调（SFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学知识图谱能为LLM的临床诊断推理提供结构化知识支撑，但如何将KG信号有效整合进LLM仍是一个未决问题，且仅凭准确率无法全面评估整合效果。

Method: 研究跨越了五种KG任务公式、三种训练范式、两个KG数据集和三个基础LLM；提出了梯度干预密度（GID）和梯度失真（GD）两个新指标，用于量化优化器对预训练模型修改的广度和几何特性，并通过控制消融实验分析目标函数和KL正则化对更新稀疏性的独立贡献。

Result: 所有范式均优于未微调基线，但准确率相近的方法在知识迁移行为上差异显著；KG判断训练结合KL正则化产生了稀疏、局部的更新（称为“手术式对齐”），而任务特定SFT产生密集更新；产生稀疏更新的范式即使域内准确率较低，也能显著提升推理质量。

Conclusion: 评估KG与LLM的整合效果不能仅依赖准确率，必须结合优化几何诊断（如GID和GD）；“手术式对齐”范式通过稀疏更新实现了更优的推理质量和知识迁移。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) offer structured medical knowledge that can ground large language model (LLM) reasoning in clinical diagnosis application, yet how KG signal should be integrated into LLMs remains an open question. We present a systematic study spanning five KG task formulations, three training paradigms, two KGs, and three base LLMs. At the task level, all paradigms improve over the non-finetuned baseline, but methods with comparable in-domain accuracy show substantially different knowledge transfer behavior. We introduce Gradient Intervention Density (GID) and Gradient Distortion (GD) to measure how broadly an optimizer modifies the pretrained model. GID and GD together reveal a clear divide: KG-judgment training under KL regularization produces sparse, localized updates (a regime we term as surgical alignment), while task-specific SFT produces dense ones. A controlled ablation shows that the objective and KL contribute to sparsity independently, and the paradigms that produce sparse updates also improve reasoning quality, even when their in-domain accuracy is lower than task-specific SFT. Assessing KG-LLM integration thus requires complementing accuracy with optimization-geometry diagnostics. Our implementation can be found at https://github.com/LARK-NLP-Lab/Surgical-Alignment.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking Clinical Decision Pathway Adherence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26592)
*Nuo Chen,Xinyang Jiang,Zilong Wang,Zhifei Zhang,Xiaoye Qu,Jiajun Deng,Yulan Guo,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEGA-CDP基准，旨在评估医疗大语言模型（LLM）遵循临床实践指南生成临床决策路径（CDP）的能力，填补了现有基准仅关注最终答案准确性的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗LLM基准主要评估最终答案的准确性，缺乏对模型遵循临床实践指南能力的有效评估，而遵循指南定义的临床决策路径（CDP）对于安全可靠的医疗决策至关重要。

Method: 通过“指南到病例”的流水线，从2,274份中英文临床实践指南中构建了包含42,353个具有明确参考CDP的临床病例数据集MEGA-CDP；支持单次对话和多次交互设置，并引入了面向CDP的路径一致性评估框架。

Result: 对16个代表性LLM的实验表明，当前模型在提供可靠的临床决策支持方面仍面临挑战，难以完全遵循指南生成决策路径。

Conclusion: 可靠的临床决策支持对当前模型而言仍具挑战性，强调了进行面向CDP评估的必要性，并证明了MEGA-CDP在推动医疗LLM遵循指南方面的价值。

Abstract: Following clinical decision pathways (CDPs) defined by clinical practice guidelines is essential for safe and reliable medical decision-making. However, existing medical large language model (LLM) benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer accuracy, providing limited evaluation of models' ability to adhere to guidelines. To address this gap, we introduce MEGA-CDP, a benchmark for evaluating whether medical LLMs can generate guideline-adherent CDPs using provided guidelines as references. MEGA-CDP is constructed from 2,274 English and Chinese clinical practice guidelines through a guideline-to-case pipeline, yielding 42,353 clinical cases with explicit reference CDPs. It supports both single-turn vignette and multi-turn interactive settings, and introduces a CDP-oriented evaluation framework for measuring pathway consistency. Experiments on 16 representative LLMs show that reliable clinical decision support remains challenging for current models, demonstrating the need for CDP-oriented evaluation and the value of MEGA-CDP for advancing guideline adherence in medical LLMs.

</details>


### [160] [Not Just Reason, Not Just Scan: Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Scientific Error Verification over Academic Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26596)
*Rongjin Li,Yuanxin Liu,Hao Zhou,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou,Xu Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VERA-RL，一种基于强化学习的科学错误检测框架，旨在解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在缺乏预设问题和证据的情况下进行自主科研验证的难题。通过构建包含12,900个样本的VERA-13K数据集及细粒度奖励机制，该方法显著提升了模型的推理可验证性，使其性能接近旗舰级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型虽具备科研辅助能力，但尚未实现完全自主的研究。主要瓶颈在于缺乏针对“无预设问题”和“无预设证据”场景下的验证任务范式及训练研究，模型难以主动审查论文、构建全局证据视图并做出可追溯的判断。

Method: 提出VERA-RL强化学习框架，遵循“推理-验证-扫描”（Reason-Verify-Scan）的渐进策略。构建了VERA-13K数据集（12,900个样本，4,300条匹配链），涵盖6类科学错误及广泛自然科学领域。设计了针对推理完整性、证据一致性和错误精确度的细粒度奖励函数，并基于Qwen3-VL-8B模型进行训练。

Result: 使用VERA-RL训练的Qwen3-VL-8B模型在可验证推理能力上取得显著提升。在“扫描”（Scan）任务上，其性能已接近Gemini 3 Pro和Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B等旗舰级多模态大语言模型。

Conclusion: VERA-RL框架有效填补了科学错误检测领域的空白，通过强化学习和专用数据集，成功推动了MLLMs向具备主动审查、证据构建及可追溯判断能力的自主科研助手转型。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly capable scientific assistants, yet they remain far from fully autonomous research. This transition requires models to actively inspect academic papers, build global evidence views, and make traceable judgments without prespecified issues or evidence. However, existing work provides limited task paradigms or training studies for such issue- and evidence-absent verification. We study this challenge through scientific error detection, where models must determine whether errors exist and justify them with evidence-based reasoning. To fill this gap, we present VERA-RL, a reinforcement-learning formulation for scientific error detection over academic papers. Following a Reason--Verify--Scan progression, we construct VERA-13K, a 12,900-sample dataset organized into 4,300 matched chains, covering 6 scientific-error categories across the research workflow and broad natural-science domains. We further introduce fine-grained rewards for reasoning completeness, evidence alignment, and error precision. Training Qwen3-VL-8B with VERA-RL substantially improves verifiable reasoning, approaching flagship MLLMs such as Gemini 3 Pro and Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B on Scan.

</details>


### [161] [Information-Guided Frontier Decoding: Contextual Utility-Driven Commitment in dMLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26641)
*Xingyou Fang,Jingxing Zhong,Xiaosong Yuan,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IGFD解码策略，通过结合置信度、邻域不确定性和结构风险优化多模态扩散模型的token提交顺序，无需额外训练即可提升解码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于置信度的解码策略优先提交局部简单token（如标点），但置信度不能反映上下文有用性，导致信息丰富的语义锚点提交滞后，削弱上下文传播并增加错误累积。

Method: 提出信息引导前沿解码（IGFD），利用token置信度、邻域不确定性和结构提交风险对候选token排序，鼓励早期提交可靠语义锚点，延迟脆弱结构token，并通过动态候选前沿限制token选择范围。

Result: 在多模态理解、推理、定位和幻觉基准测试中，IGFD在相同解码预算下，在多数基准和扩散MLLM骨干网络上均优于现有解码策略。

Conclusion: IGFD是一种无需训练、无需辅助模型或额外前向传播的解码策略，能有效优化token提交顺序，提升扩散多模态语言模型的解码质量和上下文支持。

Abstract: Decoding quality in diffusion multimodal language models (dMLLMs) depends heavily on the order in which masked tokens are committed. Existing confidence-based strategies prioritize locally easy tokens, but confidence does not necessarily reflect contextual usefulness. As a result, structurally easy tokens such as punctuation may be committed before informative semantic anchors, weakening context propagation and increasing error accumulation. We propose Information-Guided Frontier Decoding (IGFD), a training-free decoding strategy that ranks candidates using token confidence, neighborhood uncertainty, and structural commitment risk. IGFD encourages early commitment of reliable semantic anchors while delaying fragile structural tokens, improving contextual support during decoding. A dynamic candidate frontier further constrains token selection to locally expandable regions under the same decoding budget. The method requires no additional training, auxiliary models, or extra forward passes. Experiments across multimodal understanding, reasoning, grounding, and hallucination benchmarks show that IGFD consistently outperforms existing decoding strategies across the majority of benchmarks and diffusion MLLM backbones under identical decoding budgets.

</details>


### [162] [Meta-Learning Where to Allocate Experts: Task-Conditioned Layer-Wise Compression for MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26650)
*Rongfeng Wang,Shichao Weng,Zhiqiang Wang,Xinyu Liu,Yang Yi,Peilong Zhou,Hongwei Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaNet，一种支持集控制器，可在冻结MoE模型骨干、专家和路由器的情况下，动态预测每层的专家保留阈值和路由偏置，实现精度与专家激活数量的灵活权衡，显著减少平均激活专家数并保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型在部署时通常固定每层激活专家数，忽略了不同层角色、专家冗余度随深度变化以及任务难度导致的计算需求差异；现有方法要么仅离线确定层间分配，要么仅基于局部信号进行token级路由，缺乏任务级上下文感知。

Method: 提出MetaNet，一个基于支持集的控制器，为每一层预测专家保留阈值和有界路由偏置；骨干网络、专家模块和路由器保持冻结，仅通过控制器动态调整路由行为，实现任务感知的稀疏性控制。

Result: 在DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Chat上，保守设置平均激活3.61个专家（比固定k=6减少40%），MMLU精度达0.489（vs. 0.474）；激进设置平均激活2.28个专家（减少62%），精度下降约3.7个百分点；该控制器在C-Eval上无需重训练即可迁移，平均激活2.90个专家（减少52%），精度0.386。

Conclusion: MetaNet通过任务感知的动态路由控制，在显著降低计算成本的同时维持或轻微牺牲精度，实现了可调的精度-效率权衡，并具备良好的跨任务迁移能力，为高效部署MoE模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a subset of expert networks, increasing capacity while keeping per-token computation sparse. In many deployed MoEs, the number of active experts is fixed across layers and tasks, although layer roles and expert redundancy vary with depth and demand varies with difficulty. Existing approaches address only part of this setting: layer-wise allocations are usually determined offline and reused for all tasks, while token-level methods vary expert activation using local routing signals without task-level context. We propose MetaNet, a support-set controller that predicts, for each layer, an expert-retention threshold and a bounded routing bias. The backbone, experts, and router remain frozen. On DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Chat, MetaNet provides a tunable accuracy-expert-activation trade-off. Relative to fixed k=6, a conservative setting activates 3.61 experts on average (40% fewer) and achieves comparable MMLU accuracy (0.489 vs. 0.474), whereas an aggressive setting activates 2.28 experts on average (62% fewer) with accuracy approximately 3.7 percentage points lower. The MMLU-trained controller also transfers to C-Eval without retraining, activating 2.90 experts on average (52% fewer than fixed k=6) at 0.386 accuracy.

</details>


### [163] [Do LLMs Understand Personality? Rethinking Persona Fidelity Evaluation through Structured Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26674)
*Mengfan Li,Zesheng Wei,Xuanhua Shi,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型角色一致性评估中存在的“整体评估幻觉”和静态心理量表无法捕捉动态语境的问题，本文提出PRISM框架。该框架基于系统功能语言学（SFL），将评估重构为结构化逆向推理任务，从任务框架、人际立场和语言风格三个维度进行分解评估，实验证明其比传统整体评估更准确、稳定且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估范式存在两大局限：一是基于大模型的整体评估易产生“整体评估幻觉”，缺乏细粒度依据；二是静态心理测量量表无法捕捉动态对话中依赖语境的角色一致性，难以满足日益增长的角色模拟应用对高保真度的需求。

Method: 提出PRISM（基于逆向SFL建模的角色推理）框架。该方法受系统功能语言学（SFL）启发，将角色一致性分解为任务框架（Task Framing）、人际立场（Interpersonal Stance）和语言风格（Linguistic Style）三个功能维度。通过在角色条件化的标签空间上估计各维度的特定证据，并将这些信号聚合，构建了一个可解释、可审计的结构化逆向推理评估流程。

Result: 实验结果表明，PRISM在判断的准确性和稳定性上均优于传统的整体评估方法，能够提供更可靠的角色一致性评估结果，且评估过程具有可解释性。

Conclusion: PRISM通过引入心理语言学基础和结构化逆向推理，有效解决了现有评估方法的缺陷，为大语言模型角色模拟提供了一种更精准、透明且可审计的评估新范式。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed to simulate diverse human characters, ensuring persona fidelity, defined as the extent to which an agent's behavior consistently reflects the psychological and stylistic characteristics of a target persona, has become a critical requirement. However, existing evaluation paradigms primarily rely on either holistic LLM-based judges, which are prone to "holistic appraisal hallucination'', or static psychometric inventories, which fail to capture the context-dependent fidelity required in dynamic dialogue. To address these limitations, we propose PRISM (Persona Reasoning with Inverse SFL-based Modeling), a psycholinguistically grounded framework that reformulates persona fidelity evaluation as a structured inverse inference task. Inspired by Systemic Functional Linguistics (SFL), PRISM decomposes persona fidelity into three functional dimensions: Task Framing, Interpersonal Stance, and Linguistic Style. It estimates dimension-specific evidence over a persona-conditioned label space and aggregates these signals into an interpretable and auditable evaluation process. Experiments show that PRISM yields more accurate and stable judgements than traditional holistic judging, providing a more reliable framework for persona fidelity evaluation.

</details>


### [164] [FOCUS & RePAIR: Mitigating Text Degeneration via Token-Level Guidance for Pruned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26676)
*Junyoung Lee,Sehyeon Park,Shinhyoung Jang,Seonha Ryu,Hojeong Kim,Hyunsei Lee,Il Hong Suh,Yeseong Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型剪枝后出现的文本退化（特别是重复循环）问题，提出了一种基于动态过程的令牌级分析方法，并设计了FOCUS和RePAIR两种微调目标，有效减少了重复并提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝虽能压缩大语言模型，但常导致文本退化（如重复循环），即便困惑度和任务准确率未显著下降。现有研究缺乏对这一失败模式的细粒度分析，亟需理解其成因并提出针对性解决方案。

Method: 将解码过程视为动态系统，分析其如何进入并滞留于少量循环上下文；将退化分解为“循环进入风险”和“循环持续性”，指出持续性由令牌采样集中合理替代方案的“逃逸质量”控制；据此提出两种令牌级指导目标：FOCUS通过重加权蒸馏抑制高置信度区域的泄露，RePAIR利用基于起始点的正负续写对及边界损失促进合理替代方案，防止过早陷入重复。

Result: 在开放续写和指令生成任务上的实验表明，FOCUS和RePAIR均能一致地减少重复现象，并显著提升生成文本的质量。

Conclusion: 通过令牌级动态分析揭示了剪枝导致重复循环的机制，并证明通过针对性微调目标（FOCUS和RePAIR）可有效缓解该问题，为压缩模型的生成稳定性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Pruning is a practical approach to compress large language models (LLMs), but it can amplify text degeneration, especially repetition loops, even when perplexity and task accuracy remain largely unchanged. In this work, we present a token-level analysis of this failure mode by viewing decoding as a dynamical process that enters and persists in a small set of recurrent contexts. Our analysis decomposes degeneration into loop entry risk and loop persistence, and shows that persistence is controlled by the escape mass assigned to plausible alternatives within the token sampling set. Motivated by these findings, we propose two token-level guidance objectives for post-pruning fine-tuning. FOCUS reweights distillation toward high-confidence teacher regions to suppress leakage, while RePAIR uses onset-centered positive/negative continuation pairs with a margin loss to promote plausible alternatives and prevent early commitment to repetition loops. Experiments on open-ended continuation and instruction-based generation show that both methods consistently reduce repetition and improve generation quality.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Reflection: Affirmation as a Promising Behavioral Marker Associated with Quality in Text-Based Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26689)
*Michimasa Inaba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析AI辅助文本咨询中咨询师行为与对话质量的关系，发现肯定(Affirmation)比反映(Reflection)更能提升会话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度关注反映(Reflection)技术，缺乏对不同咨询师行为与对话质量关系的实证研究，需要探索更有效的咨询策略。

Method: 使用KokoroChat大规模日语文本咨询数据集，新标注咨询师策略标签和来访者痛苦程度，进行多层分析，并在ESConv英语数据集上进行跨数据集迁移实验。

Result: 肯定(Affirmation)比反映(Reflection)更一致地与会话质量相关联，该质量信号在ESConv数据集上也有一定程度的体现。

Conclusion: 研究结果为咨询师培训和情感支持系统设计提供了实证依据，已公开额外标注数据和实验源代码。

Abstract: While AI-assisted text-based counseling is gaining attention, it remains empirically unclear which counselor behaviors are associated with higher dialogue quality. Existing research often focuses heavily on Reflection, borrowing frameworks from Motivational Interviewing. To address this gap, we conduct a multi-layered analysis using KokoroChat, a large-scale Japanese text counseling dataset conducted by professional counselors and trainees, newly annotated with counselor strategy tags and client distress levels. Our results show that, under the quality indicators used in this study, Affirmation is more consistently associated with session quality than Reflection among the analyzed strategies. Cross-dataset transfer experiments further suggest that this quality signal can be observed to some extent on ESConv, an English dataset with non-expert supporters. These findings provide empirical implications for counselor training and emotional support system design. We release the additional KokoroChat annotations and experimental source code at https://github.com/UEC-InabaLab/BeyondReflection.

</details>


### [166] [Scaling phoneme-based TTS augmentation for ASR: A unified pipeline and controlled study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26697)
*Zhen Wang,TianRui Wu,RongQi Han,Hao Wu,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统一音素的TTS到ASR增强流水线，并引入音素频率引导选择（PFGS）策略，在多种语言的ASR任务中显著降低了词错误率（WER），证明了合成数据的规模、文本内容及参考语音质量是关键控制变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决合成语音用于自动语音识别（ASR）监督时，其效果高度依赖于文本选择、参考语音质量及合成数据量的问题，旨在优化TTS生成的数据以最大化ASR性能。

Method: 构建基于F5-TTS架构的多语言TTS模型，集成语言特定图素转音素、参考语音过滤、候选文本选择、合成及匹配ASR延续的流水线；提出音素频率引导选择（PFGS）算法，利用真实ASR训练标签估算的音素频率对候选句子进行排序。

Result: 在阿拉伯语、法语、意大利语和葡萄牙语的13个测试集上，PFGS在60%合成预算下优于纯真实数据训练（12个测试集）及随机选择（9个测试集），最大相对WER降低达19.3%；参考语音过滤在意大利语和法语Common Voice上分别降低绝对WER 0.29和0.59个点。

Conclusion: 合成规模、候选文本内容及参考语音质量是基于TTS的ASR增强中的关键控制变量，合理的策略选择能显著提升ASR系统的泛化能力和识别精度。

Abstract: Synthetic speech provides scalable supervision for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its benefit depends on the selected texts, reference speech, and amount of synthesized data. We present a unified phoneme-based TTS-to-ASR augmentation pipeline built around a multilingual TTS model trained from scratch using the F5-TTS architecture with language-ID conditioning. The pipeline combines language-specific grapheme-to-phoneme conversion, reference-speech filtering, candidate-text selection, synthesis, and matched ASR continuation. We further propose phoneme-frequency-guided selection (PFGS), which ranks candidate sentences using phoneme frequencies estimated from real ASR training labels. Experiments with separate monolingual ASR systems for Arabic, French, Italian, and Portuguese span 13 test sets. Across the synthesis-scale sweep, random augmentation improves over matched real-only continuation on 11 test sets. Under a nominal 60% synthesis budget, PFGS improves over real-only training on 12 test sets and over random selection on 9. Its largest relative word error rate (WER) reduction against random selection is 19.3%. With target texts and synthesis counts fixed, reference-speech filtering reduces absolute WER by 0.29 and 0.59 points on Italian and French Common Voice, respectively. These results identify synthesis scale, candidate-text content, and reference quality as important control variables in TTS-based ASR augmentation.

</details>


### [167] [PragAlign: Evidence-Sensitive Reply Assistance Across Chinese and Japanese Appropriateness Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26700)
*Xin Zhong,Satori Hachisuka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PragAlign系统，用于多语言环境下的回复辅助，通过分离上下文阅读与选择性澄清来提升回复的恰当性。实验显示，在中文评估中PragAlign显著优于基线模型；在日文评估中，虽然整体差异不显著，但PragAlign获得了最高的首选率。研究揭示了跨语言的共性与特异性判断模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言环境下的回复辅助不仅需要语言能力，还需要基于文化背景的恰当性判断，现有的方法往往难以兼顾这两点。

Method: 提出PragAlign系统，将上下文理解与选择性澄清分离。通过让9名中文母语者评估中文材料，3名日文母语者评估对应的日文材料，将其与Direct和Rule两种基线方法进行对比排名实验。

Result: 中文评估中，PragAlign的排名显著优于两个基线；日文评估中，Direct排名最低，PragAlign的首选率最高，但整体差异未达显著水平。在10个场景中有5个场景各组选择了相同的最佳条件，其中4次均选择了PragAlign。

Conclusion: 研究结果识别了跨语言回复判断中的共性与语言特异性模式，为设计支持语言和文化理解的回复辅助系统提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Reply assistance in multilingual settings requires linguistic competence and culturally situated judgments of appropriateness. We present PragAlign, which separates context reading from selective clarification, and evaluate it alongside Direct and Rule. Nine native Chinese speakers judged Chinese materials, while three native Japanese speakers judged matched Japanese versions. In the Chinese evaluation, PragAlign received significantly better ranks than both baselines. In the Japanese evaluation, Direct had the lowest mean rank, PragAlign had the highest top-rank rate, and the omnibus difference was not significant. The groups selected the same top condition in 5 of 10 scenarios, including four shared PragAlign selections. The results identify shared and language-specific judgment patterns and inform reply assistance designed to support linguistic and cultural understanding.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Expert Financial QA via Self-Improving RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26706)
*Junjie Xiong,Shawheen Ghezavat,Aum Hirpara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Improving RAG框架，通过检索、推理和评判三个专用代理的协同与反馈驱动的自我修正，解决金融问答中的数值幻觉和监管合规问题，在FinanceBench上达到86%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准单遍RAG系统缺乏基于事实的验证来捕捉数值幻觉，且无法提供监管合规所需的审计追踪，难以满足专家级金融问答的需求。

Method: 提出Self-Improving RAG框架，将文档问答分解为检索、推理和评判三个专用代理，由协调器管理并通过反馈驱动自我修正；当评判代理评分低于动态阈值时，触发重试机制，采用更广泛的检索、更谨慎的提示和放宽的接受标准等升级策略。

Result: 在FinanceBench（SEC文件问答）上，Self-Improving RAG实现86%的oracle引导准确率，Lazarus Rate达36.4%，即近40%的初始错误答案通过针对性重试得到恢复；固定检索管道配合评判驱动重试无需动态路由即可取得强结果。

Conclusion: 该框架通过记录每个决策的置信度分数实现完整可解释性，提供监管金融应用所需的审计追踪，证明了评判驱动重试机制在金融问答中的有效性。

Abstract: Expert-level financial question answering requires both grounded verification to catch numeric hallucinations and audit trails for regulatory compliance, attributes that standard single-pass RAG systems lack. We take a step toward this goal with Self-Improving RAG, a framework that decomposes document QA into three specialized agents (Retrieval, Reasoning, and Judge) coordinated by an orchestrator with feedback-driven self-correction. When the Judge Agent scores an answer below a dynamic threshold, the system triggers retry with escalated strategies: broader retrieval, more careful prompting, and relaxed acceptance criteria. We evaluate on FinanceBench (SEC filing QA), where Self-Improving RAG achieves 86% oracle-guided accuracy (measuring agreement with gold answers) with a 36.4% Lazarus Rate, recovering nearly 4 in 10 initially incorrect answers through targeted retry. A key finding is that a fixed retrieval pipeline with judge-driven retry achieves strong results without dynamic routing, providing full interpretability. Every decision is logged with confidence scores, enabling the audit trails required for regulated financial applications.

</details>


### [169] [Preserving General Capabilities during Domain Specialization with Uncertainty-Calibrated MOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26735)
*Ziyuan Liu,Jiao Ou,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Cheng Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不确定性校准的多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）方法，通过双重温度采样、正优势密度过滤和中心对数似然过滤，解决了大语言模型在垂直领域专业化过程中通用能力下降的问题，在角色扮演和医疗领域实验中显著提升了通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型专业化到垂直领域虽然能提升特定领域表现，但往往会导致推理、编码、指令遵循和创意写作等通用能力的退化。现有的多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）方法存在两个局限：常规在线策略采样很少暴露具有大正教师 - 学生优势的 token，且仅凭优势符号无法确定更新方向是否可靠。

Method: 提出不确定性校准的 MOPD 方法：1) 双重温度采样扩大候选轨迹池；2) 正优势密度过滤选择具有更强正学习信号的轨迹；3) 中心对数似然（CLL）过滤计算熵校准的教师认可分数，并根据方向 - 认可一致性概率性地保留 token 更新。

Result: 在角色扮演和医疗领域专业化实验中，该方法相比标准 MOPD 分别将通用能力平均值提升了 4.73% 和 10.84%，同时保持了垂直领域的性能。消融实验和诊断分析证实，性能提升并非源于更大的 rollout 预算，且提出的轨迹级和 token 级机制有效解决了预期的失败模式。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准的 MOPD 方法能有效缓解大语言模型领域专业化与通用能力之间的权衡问题，通过改进采样和过滤机制，在保持垂直领域性能的同时显著恢复和提升模型的通用能力。

Abstract: Specializing large language models to vertical domains improves domain-specific behavior but often degrades general capabilities such as reasoning, coding, instruction following, and creative writing. We study this domain--general trade-off in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), where a specialized student is supervised on its own sampled trajectories by domain and general teachers. Standard MOPD faces two limitations: ordinary on-policy sampling rarely exposes tokens with large positive teacher--student advantages, while the advantage sign alone does not establish whether the resulting update direction is reliable. We propose uncertainty-calibrated MOPD to address these limitations. Dual-temperature sampling broadens the candidate trajectory pool, and positive-advantage-density filtering selects trajectories with stronger positive learning signals. Centered log-likelihood (CLL) filtering then computes an entropy-calibrated teacher-endorsement score and probabilistically retains token updates according to direction--endorsement consistency. Experiments on role-playing and medical-domain specialization show that our method improves the general-capability average over standard MOPD by $4.73\%$ and $10.84\%$, respectively, while maintaining vertical-domain performance. Ablations and diagnostic analyses further confirm that the gains do not merely result from a larger rollout budget and that the proposed trajectory- and token-level mechanisms address their intended failure modes.

</details>


### [170] [Letters hide the truth from our eyes: English homophones have meaningfully different phonetic realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26749)
*Yu-Hsiang Tseng,Mirjam T. C. Ernestus,Louis F. M. ten Bosch,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现英语同音词（如weight/wait）除了时长随频率变化外，其语音实现细节也存在系统性差异，这些差异可由语境意义预测，且时间归一化语谱图是探测此类细微差异的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知英语同音词的语音时长与其使用频率相关，本研究旨在探究同音词的其他语音实现方面（除时长外）是否也存在差异。

Method: 对美国电视新闻广播中的14,000个同音词标记进行了一系列定量调查，并利用时间归一化语谱图（time-normalized spectrograms）分析语音实现的细节，以此替代传统的语音转写。

Result: 同音词对（如weight和wait）具有不同的语音实现，且这些差异可根据话语语境中的意义进行预测；即使在考虑时长相关的变异后，这些系统性差异依然存在。

Conclusion: 同音词的语音实现受语义语境影响而存在系统性差异，时间归一化语谱图是揭示这些被传统转写所掩盖的语音真相的卓越工具。

Abstract: The distribution of spoken word duration of English homophones is known to co-vary with frequency of use. This study investigates whether other aspects of the phonetic realization of homophones also differ. A series of quantitative investigations of 14,000 homophone tokens in American television news broadcasts revealed that the tokens of homophone pairs such as \textit{weight} and \textit{wait} have different phonetic realizations, and that these can be predicted from their meanings in utterance context. These systematic differences remain even when taking duration-related variation into account. Time-normalized spectrograms emerged as an excellent tool for probing the fine details of phonetic realization, and obviate the need for phonetic transcriptions, which inevitably hide the phonetic truth from our eyes.

</details>


### [171] [Equal Ranking Quality, Different Decisions: Training Order-Consistent LLM Scorers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26762)
*Markus Frohmann,Mahdiyar Alavi,Elizabeth Lingg,Navid Rekabsaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出重排序器、奖励模型和多文档问答评分器存在严重的“顺序依赖性”，即相同的排名质量下，输入顺序不同会导致最终决策（如保留集合、读者回答）显著差异。作者提出的顺序一致性监督微调（OC-SFT）方法在不降低排名质量的前提下，显著提升了决策的稳定性，优于其他针对顺序的优化目标及顺序平均蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评分模型虽然以排名质量（如nDCG）为评估标准，但其分数直接决定关键决策（阈值截断、回答生成、偏好选择）。研究发现，即使排名质量极高且相近的模型，其输出集合的重叠度也很低（0.66-0.84），表明分数的顺序依赖性导致了决策的不一致性，而现有的提示工程手段无法解决此问题。

Method: 提出顺序一致性监督微调（Order-consistency SFT, OC-SFT），通过训练使候选者的得分不再依赖于其在Prompt中的输入顺序。该方法在权重层面衰减顺序依赖性，并在三个任务（重排序、问答、偏好模型）上与针对顺序的其他优化目标及顺序平均蒸馏（order-averaged distillation）进行对比评估。

Result: OC-SFT在保持排名质量不变的情况下，在所有决策稳定性指标上均优于其他训练目标。在读者回答翻转率上，OC-SFT仅为0.125，低于其他目标的0.149-0.164。在12个基座模型上，OC-SFT比顺序平均蒸馏更稳定；单个OC-SFT排列的保留集合重叠度甚至高于十个平均化的现成排列。

Conclusion: 评估评分模型时，不能仅关注排名质量，必须报告阈值保留集合和读者回答等决策指标的稳定性。OC-SFT是解决顺序依赖性、提升决策一致性的有效方案。

Abstract: Rerankers, reward models and multi-document QA scorers score candidate documents or responses in one LLM prompt, so each score depends on their order. Such scorers are selected on ranking quality, but their scores determine a decision: what a score threshold retains, a reader answers, or a preference model selects. However, equal ranking quality does not imply equal decisions: on passage reranking, five trained scorers within 0.010 nDCG@10 retain sets that overlap by only 0.66-0.84 when reordered. A published reranker takes the highest retained-set F1 in our comparison and still overlaps by only 0.667. No prompt-time change we test removes that order dependence: the only one that gains ranking quality leaves all three decisions unchanged. Order-consistency SFT (OC-SFT) attenuates it in the weights, training a candidate's score not to depend on the order. It holds ranking quality and leads every decision-stability measure among trained scorers on all three tasks: it flips the reader's answer on 0.125 of permutation pairs against 0.149-0.164 for three other objectives that target order. It is more stable than order-averaged distillation on 12 base models, and one OC-SFT permutation retains sets that overlap more than ten averaged off-the-shelf permutations. A comparison should therefore report what a threshold retains and a reader answers, not ranking quality alone. Code is available at https://github.com/thomsonreuters/presentation-dependence.

</details>


### [172] [Instruction Quality Matters: Refining Instructions for Effective Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26779)
*Seohyeong Lee,Hwaran Lee,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出指令质量是偏好学习中的隐藏瓶颈，提出指令优化管道以提升偏好数据质量，实验表明该方法能显著改善模型对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好学习依赖响应对进行模型优化，但响应对的信息量受生成指令质量的根本性影响。低质量或模糊指令限制了响应质量分布，导致优质响应缺失和偏好信号弱化，这一关键问题此前未被充分重视。

Method: 通过 Best-of-N 和 Worst-of-N 分析验证指令质量对响应质量上下限的约束作用；设计指令优化管道，利用奖励信号筛选弱指令，并基于评分标准引导的 LLM 反馈进行修订，在不丢弃样本的前提下提升偏好数据质量。

Result: 在离线和在线偏好学习设置下，多模型与多基准测试显示，该方法相比原始数据及其他数据改进策略均取得更广泛的对齐性能提升；分析表明指令优化提高了可达到的响应质量上限，并与响应中心的偏好数据筛选方法形成互补。

Conclusion: 指令质量是决定偏好信号信息量的关键因素，通过优化指令可有效提升大语言模型对齐效果，为偏好学习数据构建提供了新视角。

Abstract: Preference learning optimizes models using response pairs, yet the informativeness of these pairs is fundamentally shaped by the instructions from which they are generated. We identify instruction quality as a hidden bottleneck in preference learning: low-quality or ambiguous instructions restrict the response-quality distribution, limiting strong chosen responses and weakening preference signals. Through Best- and Worst-of-N analyses, we show that instruction quality constrains both the ceiling and floor of sampled response quality. Motivated by this observation, we introduce an instruction-refinement pipeline that selects weak instructions using reward signals and revises them with rubric-guided LLM feedback, improving preference data without discarding examples. Across offline and online preference learning settings, experiments on multiple models and benchmarks show broad alignment improvements over original data and alternative data-improvement strategies. Further analyses indicate that instruction refinement raises achievable response quality and complements response-centric preference data curation. Overall, instruction quality emerges as a key factor governing how informative preference signals are formed for LLM alignment. Code is available at: https://github.com/01choco/instruction-refinement/

</details>


### [173] [Behavior2Trip: Towards Personalized Travel Planning via User Behavior Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26807)
*Zihao Cheng,Yingyu Shan,Hongru Wang,Zeming Liu,Xinyi Wang,Xiangrong Zhu,Yuhang Guo,Wei Lin,Yunhong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“行为感知旅行规划”新任务及Behavior2Trip基准，并开发了基于强化学习的B2T-Agent，通过挖掘用户历史行为隐式偏好来生成个性化行程，显著优于依赖显式指令的现有方法及GPT-4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划代理要么依赖用户显式指令，要么通过多轮澄清获取偏好，均忽略了用户历史行为中蕴含的丰富隐式偏好信号。这种对主动输入的过度依赖增加了交互负担，且限制了规划的个性化程度。

Method: 1. 定义新任务“行为感知旅行规划”，直接从历史行为推断偏好；2. 构建Behavior2Trip基准，包含来自大型中国在线旅游平台的11,400个实例，涵盖5个偏好维度、14个属性及平均39.8条历史行为轨迹；3. 提出B2T-Agent，一种基于强化学习的代理，利用用户行为轨迹，结合外部工具进行偏好对齐检索，并维护内部记忆模块。

Result: 在Behavior2Trip基准上，GPT-4.1在最难任务上的全约束通过率仅为0.5%；而基于Qwen3-8B构建的B2T-Agent超越了所有基线模型。此外，经B2T-Agent训练的Qwen3-8B在TravelPlanner基准上也优于GPT-4.1，展现了强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用用户历史行为轨迹进行隐式偏好推断是提升旅行规划个性化和降低交互负担的关键路径。B2T-Agent证明了基于强化学习结合记忆与工具调用的架构在处理此类复杂行为序列任务上的有效性，且具备优异的跨基准泛化性能。

Abstract: Travel planning agents assist users in generating personalized travel plans by modeling their individual preferences. Existing agents either rely on explicit user instructions or engage in multi-turn clarification to elicit user preferences. However, both approaches overlook the rich behavioral signals latent in users' past behaviors, which implicitly encode their preferences. This over-reliance on active user input increases interaction burden and limits plan personalization. To bridge this gap, we introduce a new task, Behavior-Aware Travel Planning, which infers user preferences directly from past behaviors and generates personalized travel plans. To facilitate research on this task, we introduce Behavior2Trip, a benchmark constructed from one of the largest Chinese online travel platforms, comprising 11,400 instances. Each instance represents an average of 39.8 past user behaviors spanning 14 attributes across 5 preference dimensions. We further propose B2T-Agent, a reinforcement learning-based agent that leverages user behavior trajectories, interacts with external tools for preference-aligned retrieval, and maintains an internal memory module. Experiments on Behavior2Trip show that GPT-4.1 achieves a full-constraint pass rate of only 0.5\% on the hardest tasks, while B2T-Agent built upon Qwen3-8B outperforms all baselines, highlighting the substantial challenge of this task. Moreover, Qwen3-8B trained with B2T-Agent also outperforms GPT-4.1 on the TravelPlanner benchmark, demonstrating strong generalization. Code and data are available at https://github.com/BUAA-IRIP-LLM/Behavior2Trip

</details>


### [174] [RuleWeaver: Benchmarking Rule-Centered Scenario Reasoning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26832)
*Bohan Yu,Shi-Yang Li,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleWeaver，一个用于评估大语言模型在复杂规则场景下推理能力的基准构建框架，实验显示现有模型在此任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅部分评估大语言模型在专业领域的规则推理能力，要么只关注输出级的指令约束，要么忽略了规则在场景推理中的独特作用，缺乏针对“以规则为中心的场景推理”的有效评估。

Method: 提出RuleWeaver框架：从语料库-derived的IF-THEN元规则出发，逐步将其增强为复杂规则，并将这些规则组合成以规则为中心的场景问答实例；支持基于评分标准的答案质量、规则召回率和规则精确率的过程级评估。

Result: 在11个代表性大语言模型上的实验表明，当前模型在处理复杂规则中心场景推理时仍存在困难，表现最好的模型仅获得约50%的最大评分标准分数。

Conclusion: 现有大语言模型在复杂规则驱动的场景推理能力上仍有显著不足，RuleWeaver为评估和提升该能力提供了有效的基准框架和过程级评估指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to specialized domains, where effective use of domain expertise often requires reasoning over complex rules in concrete scenarios. However, existing benchmarks only partially evaluate this capability, as they either focus on output-level instruction constraints or overlook the distinct roles that rules play in scenario reasoning. To address these gaps, this paper introduces RuleWeaver, a benchmark construction framework for evaluating rule-centered scenario reasoning. RuleWeaver starts from corpus-derived IF-THEN Meta Rules, progressively augments them into complex rules, and composes these rules into rule-centered scenario QA instances. Beyond final-answer correctness, RuleWeaver further supports process-level evaluation through rubric-based answer quality, rule recall, and rule precision. Experiments on 11 representative LLMs show that current models still struggle with complex rule-centered scenario reasoning, with even the best-performing model achieving only around 50% of the maximum rubric score. We make our code and dataset available here: https://github.com/SharkSpicy-NLP/RuleWeaver.

</details>


### [175] [Evaluating Confidence-Gated Retrieval with Matched Trajectory Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26846)
*Prateek Chhikara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“匹配轨迹回放”协议，评估置信度校准对语言模型代理决策的影响。研究发现，校准虽能显著提高已提交答案的准确率（最高41%）并降低风险，但会导致覆盖率下降和检索成本增加，且在部分数据集上整体准确率反而降低。结论表明，校准仅能优化承诺风险的可解释性，无法预估额外检索的预期收益，因此检索决策需依赖独立的信息价值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式语言模型代理通常孤立地评估置信度信号，以决定立即回答、检索证据或推迟决策，却缺乏对置信度所触发动作在轨迹层面（trajectory-level）后果的衡量。

Method: 提出“匹配轨迹回放”（matched trajectory replay）控制协议，固定候选答案状态、证据点、预算和动作成本，用于比较原始口头置信度与事后等渗校准（post-hoc isotonic calibration）。在HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，利用Mistral、GPT和Qwen模型进行多跳问答实验。

Result: 在相同承诺阈值下，校准改变了代理最终承诺回答的问题集合。在所有六个模型 - 数据集组合中，已提交答案的准确率最高提升了41个百分点；但覆盖率降低，检索使用率增加。HotpotQA整体准确率最高提升15个百分点，而MuSiQue则最高下降17个百分点。校准映射在检索深度1和2时有效，但在深度3时表现不如原始置信度。

Conclusion: 置信度校准能使承诺风险更具可解释性，但不能估计额外检索的预期收益（value-of-information）。因此，检索决策需要独立的价值或效用估计。未来的评估应报告留校校准、风险 - 覆盖率曲线及检索成本。

Abstract: Interactive language-model agents use confidence signals to decide whether to answer immediately, retrieve additional evidence (from memory or external knowledge), or defer. Yet confidence is usually evaluated in isolation, without measuring the trajectory-level consequences of the actions it triggers. We propose matched trajectory replay, a controlled protocol for comparing confidence-to-action mappings. The protocol holds candidate answer states, evidence points, budgets, and action costs fixed. We use it to compare raw verbalized confidence with post-hoc isotonic calibration in a multi-hop question-answering system using Mistral, GPT, and Qwen models on HotpotQA and MuSiQue datasets. At the same numerical commitment threshold, calibration changes which questions agents ultimately commit to answering. Across all six model-dataset pairs, it increases accuracy among committed answers by up to 41 percentage points. However, it can reduce coverage and increase retrieval use. Overall accuracy improves by up to 15 percentage points on HotpotQA but falls by up to 17 percentage points on MuSiQue. These effects reflect a shift to a more selective, lower-risk operating point, not improved answers or confidence ranking. A calibration map fitted before retrieval improves held-out calibration through retrieval depths one and two, but is worse than raw confidence at depth three for all three models. Additional evidence helps on average, but this aggregate effect does not establish whether confidence identifies which individual episodes will benefit from another retrieval. Taken together, these results show that calibration can make commitment risk interpretable, but it does not estimate the expected benefit of another retrieval. Retrieval therefore requires a separate value-of-information or utility estimate. Evaluations should report held-out calibration, risk-coverage, and retrieval cost.

</details>


### [176] [Planting a Latent Variable in Natural-Looking Text: a More Realistic Test of Belief States in LLMs and Their Link to Concept Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26887)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过在自然文本中植入可控潜在变量，实证证明了小型Transformer模型能追踪该变量的贝叶斯后验信念，并将其状态按马尔可夫链顺序在特征空间中排列成环状结构，揭示了概念几何形态由潜在变量统计动力学形成的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）被认为能追踪“信念状态”（即支配语言的潜在变量的概率分布），但此前仅在合成数据或孤立案例中得到验证，且从未将其与LLM特征几何（可解释性研究中发现的概念）进行实证关联。

Method: 利用LLM教师生成自然文本，同时在每个token处“潜意识地”沿K=8个无关的稀疏自编码器方向之一进行引导，使激活方向遵循环形马尔可夫链；随后训练小型Transformer模型学习该语料。

Result: 训练后的小型Transformer模型成功追踪了植入潜在变量的贝叶斯后验信念，并将8个状态在特征空间中精确地按马尔可夫链顺序排列成环状结构。

Conclusion: 概念的几何结构可由其背后潜在变量的统计动力学形成，为LLM内部表征的几何形态与概率推理机制之间的联系提供了实证支持。

Abstract: LLMs are thought to track "belief states," i.e., running probability distributions over the latent variables that govern language (Shai et al., 2024; Sarfati et al., 2026), but so far this has only been comprehensively demonstrated on toy synthetic data and in a few isolated case studies. It has also never been empirically connected to the geometry of LLM features (the concepts interpretability finds in model activations). In this work, we plant a controllable latent variable inside natural-looking text. An LLM teacher writes ordinary text while we "subliminally" steer it along one of K = 8 unrelated sparse autoencoder directions at each token, with the active directions following a ring-shaped Markov chain. A small transformer model trained on this corpus does indeed track the Bayesian posterior belief about our planted latent variable. Moreover, it also arranges the 8 states themselves on a ring, in the exact order of the Markov chain, which is supporting evidence that a concept's geometry can be formed by the statistical dynamics of the latent variable behind it.

</details>


### [177] [TabuLM: Morphology-Aware Tabular Pre-training for Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26923)
*Ireddi Rakshitha,Devavarapu Yashwanth,Ntakirutimana Pierre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabuLM，这是首个在卢旺达语（Kinyarwanda）表格数据上预训练的语言模型，旨在填补该语言在表格表示学习资源上的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卢旺达语是一种形态丰富的班图语，拥有超过1200万使用者，但目前缺乏专门针对该语言的表格表示学习资源，限制了其在表格相关任务上的应用。

Method: TabuLM基于KinyaBERT-large扩展，引入行、列和单元格类型嵌入以及学习到的表格结构注意力偏置；采用两个新的预训练目标：掩码单元格恢复（MCR）和列类型预测（CTP）。

Result: 在TabQA-kin基准测试上，TabuLM达到62.0%的精确匹配率，比KinyaBERT-large高出5.7个百分点，比多语言基线模型（mBERT、XLM-R）高出11.7-12.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化表格嵌入对比较和查找类问题最为关键，形态学意识提供补充增益；该研究填补了卢旺达语表格处理资源的空白，相关代码、数据和预训练模型已公开。

Abstract: We present TabuLM, the first language model pre-trained on Kinyarwanda tabular data. Kinyarwanda is a morphologically rich Bantu language spoken by over 12 million people in Rwanda, yet lacks any dedicated tabular representation learning resource. TabuLM extends KinyaBERT-large, a two-tier morphological transformer, with additive row, column, and cell-type embeddings and a learned table-structure attention bias that sharpens same-row and same-column attention. Pre-training uses two new objectives: Masked Cell Recovery (MCR), which masks entire cells and forces reconstruction from row and column context, and Column Type Prediction (CTP), which predicts column semantic types from observed cell values. We pre-train on 172 Rwandan government tables (~35,000 cells) from NISR, RAB, REB, and MoH open-data portals, and introduce TabQA-kin, the first native Kinyarwanda table question-answering benchmark comprising 526 QA pairs across 31 tables and four question types. TabuLM achieves 62.0% exact match on TabQA-kin, outperforming KinyaBERT-large by 5.7 EM points and all multilingual baselines (mBERT 49.3%, XLM-R 50.0%) by 11.7-12.7 points. Analysis shows that structural table embeddings are most decisive for comparison and lookup questions, while morphological awareness provides complementary gains. Our code, data, and pre-trained checkpoint are publicly available.

</details>


### [178] [Mapping Written Words to Spoken Words in a Different Language Using Only Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26925)
*Gabriel Pirlogeanu,Dan Oneata,Horia Cucu,Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督语音表示的对齐方法，利用视觉接地数据（图像描述）在无转录文本的情况下，学习从英语书面关键词到印地语口语实现的跨语言映射，其性能优于之前的端到端神经模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源语言环境中，收集语音数据困难，且缺乏转录文本。现有的利用图像描述收集数据的方法多依赖复杂的端到端多模态神经模型，本文旨在探索一种更简单、无需显式模型训练即可从视觉接地数据中学习跨语言词到语音映射的方法。

Method: 利用现成的图像 captioning 系统从图像中自动获取英语标签作为关键词；使用自监督语音特征对关联同一关键词的印地语语音 utterances 进行对齐；聚合对齐证据以识别对应目标词的重复语音片段；并在对齐过程中引入负样本以提升效果。

Result: 在关键词 spotting 和定位任务上的实验表明，该基于对齐的方法优于之前的基于注意力的神经模型；同时证明了在对齐过程中 Incorporating 负样本能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 研究证明了可以直接从视觉接地数据中学习跨语言词到语音的映射，无需依赖转录文本或显式的模型训练，为低资源语言的语音处理提供了高效的新途径。

Abstract: In many low-resource settings, even just eliciting speech for data collection is difficult. One promising approach has been to ask speakers to describe images. But how do we build models from such visually grounded speech data? Given a dataset of images with Hindi spoken captions, we consider how we can map a written English keyword to spoken realisations of that word in Hindi. Previous work trained end-to-end multimodal neural models. Instead, we explore a simpler alignment-based approach built on self-supervised speech representations. Written English tags are automatically obtained from images using off-the-shelf image captioning systems. Hindi utterances associated with the same keyword are then aligned (using self-supervised features), and alignment evidence is aggregated to identify recurring speech segments corresponding to the target word. Experiments evaluating keyword spotting and localization show that our alignment-based approach outperforms a previous attention-based neural model. We also show the benefit of incorporating negative examples during alignment. Our work demonstrates that cross-lingual word-to-speech mappings can be learned directly from visual grounding without transcriptions or explicit model training.

</details>


### [179] [KinyaEmbed: Contrastive Sentence Embeddings for Kinyarwanda via Multi-Stage Curriculum Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26941)
*Ireddi Rakshitha,Devavarapu Yashwanth,Ntakirutimana Pierre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KinyaEmbed，这是首个专为卢旺达语（Kinyarwanda）设计的句子嵌入模型。该模型通过四阶段课程学习策略，利用多种数据源（如法律公报、翻译对、自然语言推理三元组等）进行训练，在卢旺达语语义相似度评估和文档聚类任务上显著优于现有的多语言嵌入模型（如mE5-large和OpenAI text-embedding-3-large）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言嵌入模型（如LaBSE, mE5-large, OpenAI text-embedding-3-large）由于预训练语料中卢旺达语的代表性严重不足，导致在该语言上的表现不佳。卢旺达语是一种形态丰富的班图语，拥有超过1200万使用者，亟需专用的嵌入模型来提升其NLP任务性能。

Method: 基于KinyaBERT-large构建，采用四阶段课程学习策略，使用MultipleNegativesRankingLoss (MNRL) 进行训练：1) 利用卢旺达政府公报中的约18,000对释义数据进行多温度尺度训练；2) 在715个NLLB翻译的MNLI三元组上微调以学习蕴含结构；3) 利用English-Kinyarwanda OPUS-100翻译对对齐表示；4) 使用从KinyaCOMET中筛选出的2,936个高质量对（阈值0.8）进行精炼。最终采用七检查点集成策略（all5+23A*2，最后阶段双倍权重）。

Result: 在SemRel2024-rw基准上，KinyaEmbed的Spearman相关系数达到0.7298，比mE5-large高出20.9%，比OpenAI text-embedding-3-large高出41.0%。此外，该模型在所有评估模型中取得了最佳的文档聚类轮廓分数（0.2146）。研究还引入了新的无污染卢旺达语STS基准Wiki-RW-STS。

Conclusion: KinyaEmbed成功解决了卢旺达语在通用多语言模型中表现不佳的问题，通过针对性的数据构建和课程学习策略，显著提升了语义表示能力。该研究不仅提供了高性能的模型检查点，还公开了过滤后的训练数据对和新构建的Wiki-RW-STS基准，为卢旺达语的自然语言处理研究奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We present KinyaEmbed, the first dedicated sentence embedding model for Kinyarwanda, a morphologically rich Bantu language spoken by over 12 million people in Rwanda. Existing multilingual embedding models such as LaBSE, mE5-large, and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large perform poorly on Kinyarwanda due to severe under-representation in their pre-training corpora. KinyaEmbed is built on KinyaBERT-large and trained via a four-stage curriculum using MultipleNegativesRankingLoss (MNRL): Stage 1 leverages ~18,000 paraphrase pairs from the Official Gazette of Rwanda with three temperature scales; Stage 2 fine-tunes on 715 NLLB-translated MNLI triplets for entailment structure; Stage 3 aligns representations using English-Kinyarwanda OPUS-100 translation pairs; Stage 4 refines with 2,936 high-quality pairs filtered from KinyaCOMET at quality threshold 0.8. We evaluate on SemRel2024-rw and introduce Wiki-RW-STS, a new contamination-free Kinyarwanda STS benchmark of 300 pairs derived from Kinyarwanda Wikipedia.
