Our results generalize the past works that consider the case with full or partial observation only, and the case with full observation and side information. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effect of side information for partially observable systems.

</details>


### [217] [Knowledge Distillation Driven Semantic NOMA with GAN Refinement for 6G Robotic Vehicle Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27198)
*Qifei Wang,Zhen Gao,Li Qiao,Ziwei Wan,De Mi,Dapeng Li,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KDG-SemNOMA的框架，通过知识蒸馏和生成模型增强NOMA通信，解决了6G赋能的机器人车辆在带宽和能量受限下的高保真视觉感知问题，显著提升了像素级准确性和感知保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可持续的智能移动，6G赋能的机器人车辆需要在严格的带宽和能量约束下实现高保真视觉感知。语义通信虽然提供了频谱高效的解决方案，但在上行非正交多址接入（NOMA）机器人车辆网络中会受到严重干扰。

Method: 1. 开发基于ConvNeXt的深度联合信源信道编码（DeepJSCC）架构，带有增强注意力特征（AF）模块用于动态信道适应。2. 采用两阶段知识蒸馏策略，由正交传输教师模型指导NOMA学生模型以减轻干扰且无推理开销。3. 引入通道条件GAN（cGAN）解决像素级优化的过度平滑伪影问题，通过显式地将Stage-I初始重建和信道状态作为条件输入，将粗糙输出细化为具有真实纹理的高保真图像。

Result: 在FFHQ-256数据集上的实验表明，KDG-SemNOMA在像素级准确性和感知保真度方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: KDG-SemNOMA框架通过结合知识蒸馏和生成模型，有效解决了6G机器人车辆网络中的干扰和图像质量问题，实现了鲁棒且绿色的通信，为可持续智能移动提供了技术支持。

Abstract: To achieve sustainable intelligent mobility, 6G-empowered robotic vehicles (RVs) require high-fidelity visual perception under stringent bandwidth and energy constraints. Semantic communication offers a spectral-efficient solution but suffers from severe interference in uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) RV networks. To address this, we propose a knowledge distillation-driven and generative models-enhanced NOMA framework for robust and green RV communications, named KDG-SemNOMA. First, we develop a ConvNeXt-based deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) architecture with an enhanced attention feature (AF) module for dynamic channel adaptation. Second, to mitigate interference without inference overhead, an orthogonal transmission teacher model guides the NOMA student model via a two-stage knowledge distillation strategy. Finally, to address the over-smoothing artifacts of pixel-wise optimization, we introduce a channel-conditional GAN (cGAN). By explicitly taking the Stage-I initial reconstruction and channel states as conditional inputs, this module refines coarse outputs into high-fidelity images with realistic textures. Experiments on FFHQ-256 demonstrate that KDG-SemNOMA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pixel-level accuracy and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [218] [Real-Time Reconstruction of Markov Sources over MPR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27116)
*Pansee S. Elessawy,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具备多包接收（MPR）能力的共享无线信道上，两个二元马尔可夫源的实时重构与远程致动问题，旨在通过优化采样策略最小化加权实时重构误差（RTE）或致动误差成本（CAE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于冲突丢弃机制，忽视了同时传输的可能性；本文旨在利用MPR特性，以任务导向的重构和致动误差而非原始交付率来评估通信性能，并在传感器独立随机采样的非凸约束下寻找最优策略。

Method: 首先推导RTE和CAE关于有效更新概率的闭式表达；利用可达成更新速率区域的几何特性，将帕累托有效独立随机策略的搜索简化为有限个一维边界分支搜索；同时引入时间共享机制作为基准，证明仅需两个帕累托极端模式即可表征最优性能，以此量化独立随机化带来的性能损失。

Result: 理论分析表明，当传感器独立随机化时，MPR诱导的更新速率映射呈双线性，导致优化问题非凸，但可通过几何方法高效求解；数值结果显示，仅具备MPR能力并不足以提升性能，只有当双源并发接收可靠时，同时解码才能优化任务目标，否则避免同时传输的策略同样有效。

Conclusion: MPR技术的应用效果高度依赖于并发接收的可靠性；在特定信道条件下，精心设计的避免冲突策略可能优于利用MPR的同时传输策略，这为面向任务的无线传感网络采样策略设计提供了新的理论依据和优化范式。

Abstract: This paper studies the real-time reconstruction and remote actuation of two binary Markov sources over a shared wireless channel with multi-packet reception (MPR). Unlike many existing collision-based formulations that discard simulta- neous transmissions, we exploit MPR and evaluate communica- tion through reconstruction and actuation errors rather than raw delivery rates. We consider two sensors observing the sources and aim to find sampling policies that minimize the weighted real- time reconstruction error (RTE), or equivalently the weighted cost of actuation error (CAE) for the considered binary sources, under per-sensor sampling constraints. We first obtain closed- form expressions for the RTE and CAE in terms of the effective update probabilities. When the sensors randomize independently, the MPR-induced update-rate map becomes bilinear, and the constrained optimization is nonconvex. We exploit the geometry of the achievable update-rate region to show that the search over Pareto-efficient independently randomized policies reduces to a finite set of one-dimensional boundary-branch searches with closed-form candidates. As a benchmark, we allow time sharing among joint sensor actions, and we prove that at most two Pareto- extreme modes are sufficient, and use this benchmark to quantify the loss caused by independent randomization. Numerical results reveal that MPR capability alone is not sufficient; simultaneous decoding improves the task-oriented objective only when con- current reception is reliable for both sources; otherwise, policies that avoid simultaneous transmissions can be equally effective.

</details>


### [219] [Deterministic Identification over Additive Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27243)
*Jonathan E. W. Huffmann,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对加性高斯信道（特别是AWGN信道）中尚未确定的确定性识别容量问题，引入格理论（Lattice Theory）新视角，建立了紧密的容量上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代通信系统对数据速率、可靠性和能效要求严苛，基于信道的识别（Identification via channels）作为后香农信息理论的新范式，能提供远超传统信道编码的识别速率。确定性识别因在实现复杂度与通信增益间取得平衡，对未来系统（如分子通信）至关重要。然而，即便是最简单的加性白高斯信道（AWGN），其确定性识别容量至今未知，亟需理论突破。

Method: 提出关于确定性识别的新视角，并应用格理论（Lattice Theory）的相关结果进行数学推导与分析。

Result: 成功建立了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量的紧密界限（tight bounds），填补了该领域的理论空白。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅解决了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量未知的难题，还为未来高需求通信系统提供了重要的理论依据和性能分析工具。

Abstract: Modern communication systems impose strict demands on data rate,
  reliability, and power efficiency. In this context, emerging communication
  paradigms such as identification via channels have become an important topic
  in post-Shannon information theory, offering the potential for substantially
  higher identification rates than in conventional channel coding.Deterministic
  identification is particularly interesting for specialized communication
  scenarios because it provides a balance between implementation complexity and
  the communication gains due to higher identification rates. It is therefore a
  promising communication scheme for future communication systems, including
  molecular communication systems.
  Additive Gaussian channels, particularly the additive white Gaussian channel,
  are among the most important channel models for analyzing the performance of
  communication systems in information and communication theory. This importance
  stems from both their mathematical tractability and their ubiquitous
  appearance in practical applications.
  To date the deterministic identification capacity for additive Gaussian
  channels remains unknown even for the simplest case of the additive white
  Gaussian channel. In this paper, we establish tight bounds on the
  deterministic identification capacity of additive Gaussian channels by
  introducing a new perspective on deterministic identification. To this end, we
  apply results from lattice theory to obtain new capacity results.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [TRACE: Retrospective Streaming Generation of Physical Fields under Sparse Structured Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26219)
*Xinyu Zhang,Lihao Chen,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE，一种用于结构化传感下物理场重建的回顾性流式生成框架，通过结合潜在空间贝叶斯推断、卡尔曼滤波和回顾性平滑，在稀疏观测下实现了优于现有方法的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式重建方法主要假设固定的批量条件输入，无法有效处理真实传感系统中常见的结构化数据流（如局部扫描、移动视场观测或帧缺失），导致在时序稀疏和空间局部化传感协议下表现不佳。

Method: TRACE框架在学习到的连续坐标潜在空间中执行近似贝叶斯推断：首先将稀疏的非网格测量转换为生成性潜在证据，随后通过类卡尔曼滤波将其与状态空间时序先验融合，最后利用回顾性平滑对观测不足的过去帧进行修正。

Result: 在活性物质、海洋声速场和超新星模拟的实验表明，TRACE在时序稀疏和空间局部化传感协议下的重建质量上，匹配或超越了逐帧生成重建器、离线时空方法以及流式数据同化基线。

Conclusion: TRACE成功解决了结构化传感流下的物理场重建难题，证明了将生成式先验与流式贝叶斯推断及回顾性平滑相结合，能显著提升在复杂观测条件下的重建性能，为科学监测和数字孪生构建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is central to scientific monitoring, inverse modeling, and digital-twin construction. Generative reconstruction has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for this task by learning data-driven physical priors that complete plausible full fields from limited observations. However, existing methods largely assume fixed, batch conditioning, whereas real sensing systems often produce structured streams: probes scan local regions, instruments observe moving fields of view, and communication constraints may leave entire frames missing. We propose TRACE, a retrospective streaming generative reconstruction framework for physical fields under structured sensing. TRACE performs approximate Bayesian inference in a learned continuous-coordinate latent space, converting sparse off-grid measurements into generative latent evidence, fusing it with a state-space temporal prior through Kalman-style filtering, and refining under-observed past frames via retrospective smoothing. Experiments on active matter, ocean sound-speed fields, and supernova simulations show that TRACE matches or surpasses frame-wise generative reconstructors, offline spatiotemporal methods, and streaming data-assimilation baselines in reconstruction quality under temporally sparse and spatially localized sensing protocols.

</details>


### [221] [A Unified Descriptive-Complexity Framework for Model Selection under Correlated Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26618)
*Yanhang Zhang,Wei Liu,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出描述复杂性信息准则（DCIC），通过Kraft可容许码长正则化大候选模型集，在强预测依赖和模型类不确定性下实现选择一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强预测依赖和模型类不确定性下的模型选择挑战，特别是当存在指数级数量模型时，现有方法依赖RIP型条件或无法处理模型误设。

Method: 提出DCIC准则，基于编码原理统一异质类复杂性尺度，建立近似误差分离理论，开发复杂度引导搜索路径以平衡计算与统计权衡。

Result: 在次Weibull噪声下证明选择一致性，获得非渐近Oracle风险界，实现类-模型恢复和跨类风险适应，数值实验显示稳定支持恢复和良好估计性能。

Conclusion: DCIC通过编码原理有效处理强依赖和模型类不确定性，提供理论保证和实用搜索策略，在模型误设下仍保持良好性能。

Abstract: Model selection becomes particularly challenging under strong predictor dependence and model-class uncertainty, especially when there are exponentially many models. We propose a Descriptive-Complexity Information Criterion (DCIC) that regularizes large candidate model collections through Kraft-admissible code lengths. Under sub-Weibull noise, we establish selection consistency through approximation-error separation without relying on RIP-type conditions, together with nonasymptotic oracle risk bounds that remain valid under model misspecification. The same coding principle places heterogeneous classes on a common complexity scale at a small additional class-identification cost. This extension yields class--model recovery under suitable identifiability conditions and risk adaptation across classes. We further develop a complexity-guided search path that makes the computation--statistics trade-off explicit. Large penalties yield polynomial-size retained search regions with high probability, whereas smaller penalties sharpen the oracle risk benchmark. Numerical experiments illustrate stable support recovery and favorable estimation performance under strong dependence and model-class uncertainty.

</details>


### [222] [Incremental Recommendation via Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26804)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,David Gustafsson,Michael O'Riordan,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果架构的推荐系统，通过使用已有的holdback数据，解决了推荐资源浪费的问题。通过双阈值策略，在减少7%推荐展示的同时，保持了用户消费内容量不变，并提升了模型校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐展示是有限资源，向用户推荐其本会自然发现的内容没有增量价值，且会挤占其他推荐机会。现有模型未考虑这一因果效应，导致资源浪费。

Method: 扩展现有生产推荐模型为因果架构，利用已有的holdback实验数据；针对处理组和holdback组归因窗口不匹配问题，提出双阈值目标策略，仅在高处理概率和低自然发现概率时进行推荐。

Result: 在Spotify百万用户规模的A/B测试中，该策略减少了7%的推荐展示量，且整体推荐内容消费量无显著下降；联合训练holdback数据提升了处理头的校准能力。

Conclusion: 因果模型能学习到比仅用观测数据训练的模型更具泛化性的表征，通过holdback数据优化推荐系统可在不损害用户体验的前提下提高资源利用效率。

Abstract: Recommendation impressions are a finite resource, hence delivering a recommendation to a user who would discover the content organically yields no incremental value and displaces other recommendations that could. We address this by extending an existing production recommendation model to a causal architecture using holdback data that is already collected as part of routine experimentation infrastructure, requiring no new data collection. A central challenge is that attribution windows differ between treated and holdback observations: treated users are attributed a stream within a short direct-response window, while holdback users are attributed organic streams over a multi-day window. This mismatch makes naive treatment-effect subtraction invalid. We resolve this with a dual-threshold targeting policy that delivers a recommendation only when the probability of a treated stream is high and the probability of organic stream is low. In a production-scale A/B test on millions of Spotify users, this policy reduces recommendation impressions by 7% with no statistically significant reduction in overall recommended content consumption. We further show that joint training with holdback data improves calibration of the treated head relative to the production baseline, and argue this can be taken as evidence that causal models learn more generalisable representations than models trained on observational data alone.

</details>


### [223] [Why not to use the Gaussian kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26974)
*Toni Karvonen,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文认为高斯核极其脆弱，不应作为默认选择，因其导致条件方差过小引发过度自信，并造成数值病态问题，根源在于其解析性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核在回归和分类任务中广泛使用，但其存在严重缺陷，需要指出其潜在风险并建议避免使用。

Method: 通过分析高斯核的条件方差特性和数值稳定性，论证其解析性导致的问题。

Result: 高斯核产生不切实际的小条件方差，导致预测不确定性量化时出现灾难性过度自信；同时引发数值病态，需使用nugget项等技巧修正模型。

Conclusion: 应避免使用解析核（包括高斯核），因其解析性导致指数级谱密度衰减，引发上述问题。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [224] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究指出，基于列置换的平行分析（Parallel Analysis）在语言模型隐藏坐标分析中违反了函数保持的重新参数化不变性，导致组件数量判定结果不稳定且依赖于隐含坐标的选择，而正交不变比较器分数则表现出数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的隐藏坐标并非由其输入输出函数唯一确定，因此基于表示的测量方法应对保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性。然而，现有的列置换平行分析方法可能因参考分布和选定组件数量的变化而违反这一原则，即使模型函数和观测协方差谱保持不变。

Method: 研究在五个模型、三个检索域和75种变换下进行了实证分析，对比了列置换平行分析与仅中心化控制组及独立平行分析种子下的组件计数差异，并评估了正交不变比较器分数的稳定性。

Result: 实证结果显示，组件计数的中位数分歧为0.79，固定阈值决策的中位数分歧为0.26。在仅中心化的控制实验中，尽管观测谱未变，1200次中有1141次组件计数发生改变；而独立平行分析种子未改变任何决策。相比之下，正交不变比较器分数在保持类似判别能力的同时数值稳定。

Conclusion: 平行分析导出的组件计数和决策可能反映的是隐藏坐标的选择而非模型的固有属性，因此在使用表示衍生测量时，应优先采用具有正交不变性的方法以确保结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [225] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动扩散的逆问题求解器，通过迭代检测和纠正模型错误指定，即使在初始训练边界排除真实参数的情况下，也能发现并学习参数空间的正确区域，为自适应域增强提供了贝叶斯理论依据，并在玩具逆问题和量子色动力学分析中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程应用中从实验可观测数据估计未知参数的逆问题具有非线性、噪声和不适定性等挑战，且在先验知识不完整的情况下难以进行鲁棒推断。

Method: 训练扩散模型（DM）学习参数空间与可观测空间之间的映射，通过后验不确定性迭代检测和纠正模型错误指定，实现自适应域增强。

Result: 在具有无限解的玩具逆问题和量子色动力学中核子结构分析的量子关联函数参数化事件中，验证了该逆问题求解器的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为不完整先验知识下的逆问题提供了 principled 的贝叶斯 justification，确保了鲁棒推断。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [226] [Recovering Expert Critic-Sourced Network Adjacency between Musical Artists from Acoustic Distributions: A Construct-Validity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27291)
*Elena Badillo-Goicoechea,Fengfeng He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了音乐推荐中的关键邻近性信号，通过声学内容验证其有效性，发现其在冷启动场景下具有较高的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐依赖用户交互和内容信号，但冷启动问题突出。关键邻近性（专家评论中艺术家间的关联）作为第三信号，缺乏外部验证，需确认其是否基于音乐本身而非社会背景。

Method: 将艺术家表示为80个低层Essentia声学描述符的经验分布，使用边缘最优传输（Wasserstein）距离建模成对邻近性，在冷启动、艺术家不重叠的分割下评估关键邻近性的声学可恢复性。

Result: 集成模型在样本外AUC达到0.767（95% CI 0.761-0.775），随关键共识增加而提升，多源证实边达0.865。分层评估显示，紧密界定的场景类流派比宽泛行业术语具有更高可恢复性。

Conclusion: 关键话语是推荐的丰富信息源，可分解为可复现的“声学核心”和由叙事定位等驱动的“社会剩余”，提供可扩展的冷启动发现机制及社会学基础的音乐信息检索研究方法。

Abstract: Music recommendation relies primarily on two signals: user-item interactions, which fail in the cold-start regime, and intrinsic musical content, available for any recording. We argue that a third, largely untapped signal is both richer and more principled: critical adjacency, the pairwise relation established when an expert critic explicitly links two artists in long-form prose. It encodes deliberate judgments about which artists belong together. Prior work established its internal validity, showing it recovers coherent, interpretable communities and can match collaborative filtering in user-satisfaction simulations, with no user data. What has been missing is external validation: whether this critic-sourced relation is grounded in the music itself versus sociological context. We test it against acoustic content, reframing the question as one of construct validity.
  Representing artists as empirical distributions over 80 low-level Essentia acoustic descriptors and modeling pairwise proximity via marginal optimal-transport (Wasserstein) distances, we evaluate how far critical adjacency is sonically recoverable under a cold-start, artist-disjoint split. Our ensemble recovers these edges at out-of-sample AUC of 0.767 (95% CI 0.761-0.775). Recoverability rises monotonically with critical consensus, reaching 0.865 on multi-source attested edges. Stratified evaluations align with sociological models of genre: tightly bounded, scene-based genres show higher recoverability than broad industry umbrella terms.
  Critical discourse is thus a rich source of information for recommendation, decomposing into a reproducible "sonic core" and a "sociological remainder" driven by narrative positioning, subcultural context, and canonical placement. The work offers both a scalable cold-start discovery mechanism and a sociologically grounded approach to MIR and MRS research.

</details>


### [227] [A Finite Sample Analysis for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27313)
*Zijie Cheng,Xiang Li,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了同步分位数时序差分学习（QTD）在表格型分布强化学习中的全局有限样本保证，分离了两种稳定性机制，并分析了局部随机波动与全局样本复杂度的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为同步分位数时序差分学习（QTD）提供全局有限样本保证，区分局部随机波动和全局样本复杂度，解决现有理论中缺乏对QTD算法收敛性和样本复杂性的严格分析的问题。

Method: 通过全局比较论证（基于奖励累积分布函数的序单调性和分布贝尔曼算子的$W_\infty$收缩性）将任意初始迭代带入局部邻域，然后在该邻域内线性化QTD平均场，利用其雅可比矩阵为非奇异M矩阵及关联正半群进行方差敏感鞅分析。

Result: 对于步长$α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$（$a\in(1/2,1)$），主导的最后迭代波动阶为$\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$，且对分位数数量无多项式依赖；确定性瞬态和所需预热仍依赖于最小贝尔曼目标密度（最坏情况下阶为$m^{-1}$）。

Conclusion: 该结果清晰区分了局部随机波动和全局样本复杂度，为QTD算法提供了严格的理论保证，表明其在分布强化学习中的有效性和稳定性。

Abstract: We establish a global finite-sample guarantee for synchronous quantile temporal-difference learning (QTD) in tabular distributional reinforcement learning. The proof separates two stability mechanisms. A global comparison argument, based on the order monotonicity of reward cumulative distribution functions and the $W_\infty$ contraction of the distributional Bellman operator, brings an arbitrarily initialized iterate into a local neighborhood. Inside that neighborhood, we linearize the QTD mean field. Its Jacobian is a nonsingular $M$-matrix, and the associated positive semigroup permits a variance-sensitive martingale analysis. For stepsizes $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ with $a\in(1/2,1)$, the leading last-iterate fluctuation is of order $\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$ and has no polynomial dependence on the number of quantiles. The deterministic transient and the required burn-in can still depend on the smallest Bellman-target density, which is of order $m^{-1}$ in the worst case. The result therefore distinguishes sharply between the local stochastic fluctuation and the global sample complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Buy-at-Bulk Facility Location on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26337)
*Shamisa Nematollahi,Daniel Vaz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了买量设施选址问题（BBFL）及其变体kCFL在树形实例上的近似算法。针对单位需求和可拆分需求，提出了PTAS；针对电缆不可拆分需求，证明了星形图上的NP-hardness，并给出了树形图上的资源增强近似算法及2-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题结合了经典设施选址与买量网络设计，源于电信网络的实际应用需求，旨在优化设施开启成本与基于容量成本函数的边传输成本之和。

Method: 针对树形实例，分别对单位需求/可拆分变体设计多项式时间近似方案（PTAS）；对电缆不可拆分变体（kCFL），先证明其在星形图上无法以优于3/2的因子近似，随后提出一种允许每条电缆容量超额(1+ε)倍但总成本最优的算法，并据此推导出标准的2-近似算法。

Result: 1. BBFL在单位需求和可拆分变体下存在PTAS；2. kCFL在电缆不可拆分设定下，星形图上的近似下界为3/2；3. 提出了树形实例下成本最优但容量超额(1+ε)的算法；4. 证明了kCFL在树上存在2-近似算法。

Conclusion: 在树形拓扑结构下，买量设施选址问题及其电缆不可拆分变体具有良好的近似可解性，通过资源增强技术可有效平衡容量限制与成本优化，为相关网络设计问题提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We consider the buy-at-bulk facility location problem (BBFL), a problem combining the classic facility location problem with buy-at-bulk network design, which finds motivation in telecommunication networks. In it, we are given a graph with edge lengths, opening costs and demands for each vertex, and a monotone and subadditive capacity-cost function, and our task is to open facilities on a subset of the vertices and route the demand from each vertex to these facilities. The cost of a solution (which we want to minimize) is given by the opening costs of the chosen facilities, plus the cost on each edge, which is given by its length times the cost of providing enough capacity for the demands through the edge, given by the capacity-cost function. A common variant of the problem, the $k$-cable facility location problem (kCFL), considers the case where capacity is provided by buying copies of given cable types, each with a certain capacity and cost.
  We study BBFL on tree instances and show, for the unit-demand and splittable variants, that the problem admits a PTAS (a $(1+ε)$-approximation for any $ε> 0$). We also consider kCFL in the new setting of cable-unsplittable demands, where the demand of a vertex cannot be split among multiple cables. We show that the problem is NP-hard to approximate to a factor better than $3/2$ on stars, and then provide an algorithm for tree instances that outputs a solution with optimal cost, but which exceeds the capacity on each cable by a factor of $1+ε$. As a consequence, we show that the problem has a $2$-approximation algorithm on trees.

</details>


### [229] [The Time-Dependent Traveling Salesman Problem with Loose Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26360)
*Francisco J. Soulignac*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解带时间窗的时变旅行商问题（TDTSPTW）的精确框架，通过结合动态规划标记算法、列生成、ng-记忆增强和精确搜索，显著提升了可求解实例的规模和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的带时间窗旅行商问题（TSP-TW）未考虑交通拥堵对行驶时间的影响，而时变旅行商问题（TDTSPTW）更贴近实际，但现有方法在求解大规模实例或宽松时间窗情况下存在局限性，需要更高效的精确求解框架。

Method: 开发了一个基于分支定价（branch-and-price）的精确框架，核心包括动态规划标记算法、列生成、ng-记忆增强技术，并利用完成界限（completion bounds）进行状态空间稀疏化、变量固定和精确搜索剪枝。

Result: 该框架在包含超过10,000个实例的基准测试中，成功求解了所有包含最多45个客户的实例，包括所有无时间窗且最多50个客户的实例，尤其在宽松时间窗条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架显著扩展了TDTSPTW问题可精确求解的实例范围，在各种时间窗紧度下均保持高效，为实际物流和调度问题提供了强有力的优化工具。

Abstract: The time-dependent traveling salesman problem with time windows (TDTSPTW) generalizes the well-known traveling salesman problem with time windows by accounting the effects of congestion on travel times. In this paper, we develop an exact framework for the TDTSPTW with a makespan objective that extends the range of instances solvable to optimality under loose time windows while remaining effective across all levels of time-window tightness. Our framework relies on a dynamic-programming labeling algorithm and combines column generation, ng-memory augmentation, and exact search, using completion bounds for state-space sparsification, variable fixing, and exact search pruning. Embedded within a branch-and-price method, the framework solves all instances with up to 45 customers in a benchmark comprising more than 10,000 instances, including all instances without time windows with up to 50 customers.

</details>


### [230] [Unpublished Draft: A Post-Processing Approach to Fairness in Tie-Aware Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26478)
*Somya Nigam,Johan Springael,Kenneth Sörensen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后处理方法，用于在给定不公平的感知平局共识排名时，找到最接近的公平共识排名，填补了现有研究主要关注严格排序排列而忽视实际中常见的平局情况的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平排名聚合研究大多假设输入和输出排名为严格排序的排列，但实际应用中带有平局的排名更为常见，因此需要解决这一差距。

Method: 提出了一种精确算法和一种快速启发式方法，用于从给定的不公平感知平局共识排名中确定最接近的公平共识排名。

Result: （摘要中未明确说明具体实验结果，但暗示方法能有效找到最接近的公平共识排名）

Conclusion: 该研究通过提供后处理方法填补了公平排名聚合中处理平局情况的空白，为实际应用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of finding a fair consensus ranking is an active research topic in the domain of fair rank aggregation and has been well studied; however, existing studies predominantly consider both the input rankings and the output ranking to be permutations where elements are always strictly ordered. In practice, however, a ranking with ties is far more common. This study bridges this gap by presenting a post-processing approach to determine the closest fair consensus ranking when an unfair tie-aware consensus ranking is provided. It proposes an exact algorithm and a fast heuristic to achieve this.

</details>


### [231] [Cheaper by the Batch: Shared Traversal for Genotype Graph Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26488)
*Aaron Li,Yifan Li,Drew DeHaas,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种批量突变重映射算法，通过单次共享的反向拓扑遍历替代独立的遍历，在保持精确载体集语义的同时，比独立重映射快至10.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体遗传学中，基因型表示图（GRG）通过共享子图结构无损编码大规模样本的相位遗传变异。更新突变是一个结构性编辑问题，现有方法逐个重映射突变，效率较低。

Method: 引入批量突变重映射算法，采用单次共享的反向拓扑遍历，传播紧凑的位并行每突变状态，并使用自适应稀疏/密集载体集表示，涵盖从稀有到常见变异密度。

Result: 在受控更新工作负载和端到端等位基因极化（群体遗传分析中常见的批量载体集更新）上评估，该方法比独立重映射快至10.5倍，同时保持精确的载体集语义。

Conclusion: 批量处理是内存可扩展的，与基于分割的并行性（每个工作线程复制图和遍历状态）互补，显著提升了GRG更新效率。

Abstract: Updating a graph by inserting or replacing nodes while preserving semantics and reusing existing structure is a recurring computational problem. In population genetics, this problem arises in the genotype representation graph (GRG), a directed acyclic graph that losslessly encodes phased genetic variation across hundreds of thousands of samples by sharing subgraph structure for individual mutations. In a GRG, each mutation's carrier set is implicitly encoded as the set of leaf nodes reachable from the node it is assigned to. Updating a mutation is therefore a structural editing problem, and current approaches remap mutations individually.
  This paper introduces a batched mutation-remapping algorithm that replaces independent reuse-aware traversals with a single shared reverse-topological pass, identifying reuse candidates for an entire batch at once. The pass propagates compact bit-parallel per-mutation state and uses an adaptive sparse/dense carrier set representation spanning rare-to-common variant densities. Batching is the memory-scalable complement to split-based parallelism, which instead replicates graph and traversal state per worker. Our remapping is evaluated on a controlled update workload and on end-to-end allele polarization, a bulk carrier set update that is common in population genetic analysis. Our approach is up to 10.5$\times$ faster than independent remapping while preserving exact carrier-set semantics.

</details>


### [232] [Hadamard Flattening and Gaussian Pooling Sketch for Least Squares with Coordinate-wise Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26552)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的快速稠密随机变换算法，解决了超定$\ell_2$回归中解向量$\ell_\infty$范数精度保证的问题。该算法通过结合随机Hadamard展平、随机置换和平衡不相交高斯池化，实现了$O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log d)$的行数复杂度，并修正了前人工作中关于独立性假设的错误，同时保持了接近线性的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的子空间嵌入虽能保持回归成本，但难以保证解向量在$\ell_\infty$范数下的坐标级精度。前人（Song et al.）声称改进了行数复杂度至$O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log^3 n)$，但其证明依赖于一个在一般情况下不成立的独立性假设。本文旨在构建一个真正具有接近线性行数复杂度且理论严谨的算法，以填补这一理论漏洞并实现高效的$\ell_\infty$精度保证。

Method: 提出一种新的快速稠密随机变换（fast, dense randomized transform），该变换由三个关键步骤组成：随机Hadamard展平（randomized Hadamard flattening）、随机置换（random permutation）以及平衡不相交高斯池化（balanced, disjoint Gaussian pooling）。该方法的核心在于，在给定Hadamard和置换阶段后，草图问题转化为精确的高斯回归，且噪声与整个草图设计矩阵条件独立，从而解决了前人证明中缺失的独立性条件。

Result: 新算法在$m=O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log d)$行数的情况下实现了所需的$\ell_\infty$范数误差保证。计算效率方面，仅需一次Hadamard传递，内部填充维度为$N=\widetilde{O}(n+\varepsilon^{-2}d^3)$，计算草图对$(SA, Sb)$的时间复杂度为$O(Nd\log N)=\widetilde{O}(nd+\varepsilon^{-2}d^4)$。此外，文章还给出了一个显式反例，证明了前人工作中独立性假设的失效。

Conclusion: 通过引入条件独立性机制，本文成功构建了具有严格理论保证且行数复杂度接近$d$的随机草图算法。该方法不仅修正了现有文献中的理论缺陷，还提供了一个在实际应用中高效可行的解决方案，显著推进了过约束$\ell_2$回归中高精度解向量的计算理论。

Abstract: Randomized sketch-and-solve algorithms accelerate overconstrained $\ell_2$ regression by replacing the input with a smaller problem. Standard subspace embeddings guarantee that the cost of the regression is nearly preserved, but coordinate-wise accuracy of the solution is more delicate: we want the solution vector itself to be close to the optimal solution in $\ell_\infty$ norm. In particular, we want to find a vector $x'\in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $\|x'-x^*\|_\infty\leq \fracε{\sqrt d}\cdot \|Ax^\star-b\|_2\cdot \|A^\dagger\|_{\rm op}$. Price, Song and Woodruff initiated the study of this problem and showed that the subsampled randomized Hadamard transform (SRHT) with $O(ε^{-2} d^{1+Θ(\sqrt{\log\log n/\log d})})$ rows achieves this guarantee. A subsequent work of Song, Ye, Yin and Zhang claimed to improve the row count to $O(ε^{-2}d\log^3 n)$. Unfortunately, their proof relies on an independence assumption that does not hold in general, and we exhibit an explicit instance on which it fails.
  To achieve a truly nearly-linear-in-$d$ row count, we introduce a new fast, dense randomized transform, which combines a randomized Hadamard flattening, a random permutation, and balanced, disjoint Gaussian pooling. Conditioned on the Hadamard-and-permutation stage, the sketched problem becomes an exact Gaussian regression in which the noise is independent of the entire sketched design; this conditional independence is exactly what the earlier argument was missing. Our sketch yields the $\ell_\infty$ guarantee with $m=O(ε^{-2}d\log d)$ rows, uses one Hadamard pass with a padded internal dimension $N=\widetilde{O}(n+ε^{-2}d^3)$, and is efficient to apply: the sketched pair $(SA, Sb)$ can be computed in $O(Nd\log N)=\widetilde{O}(nd+ε^{-2}d^4)$ time.

</details>


### [233] [On the Instance Optimality of Bidirectional Dijkstra's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26952)
*Matic Požar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文指出了现有Dijkstra算法实例最优性分析中的缺陷，提出了一种改进的双向Dijkstra变体以在加权图中实现实例最优性，并重新探讨了无权图及简单图中的实例最优性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Haeupler等人关于最短路径算法实例最优性研究的启发，旨在解决标准查询模型下最短$st$-路径算法（特别是单向和双向Dijkstra算法）在实例最优性分析中存在的错误和遗漏。

Method: 1. 构建反例以揭示现有分析的缺陷；2. 提出并证明一种双向Dijkstra算法的最小简单修改变体；3. 简化无权图下的下界证明；4. 针对简单图建立基于节点数$n$、查询边数$m$及最大度$\Delta$的实例最优性条件。

Result: 1. 发现了单向和双向Dijkstra算法实例最优性分析中的若干问题并提供反例；2. 证明了修改后的双向Dijkstra变体在加权设置下具有实例最优性；3. 确认无权图中不存在优于$O(\Delta)$因子的实例最优算法；4. 在满足$n\ge m/16$或最大度$\le \sqrt{m}$的简单图实例中，实现了常数因子内的实例最优性。

Conclusion: 通过修正理论缺陷和提出新变体，本文确立了加权图中双向搜索的实例最优性，明确了无权图的性能界限，并在特定条件的简单图中取得了实例最优性的实质性进展。

Abstract: Recent work by Haeupler, Hladík, Rozhon, Tarjan, and Tětek on the instance optimality of shortest-path algorithms established several results concerning Dijkstra's algorithm and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithm in weighted and unweighted graphs. Motivated by these results, we revisit the question of instance optimality for shortest $st$-path algorithms in the standard query model.
  We identify several issues in the analysis of the instance optimality of both unidirectional and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithms and provide corresponding counterexamples. We then propose a minimal simple modification of the bidirectional Dijkstra algorithm and prove that the resulting variant is instance optimal in the weighted setting. Furthermore, we revisit the unweighted case, provide a simplified proof of the lower bound showing that no algorithm can achieve instance optimality up to a factor better than $O(Δ)$, where $Δ$ denotes the maximum degree of the graph, and discuss the implications of this result for approximation algorithms. Finally, we make progress on the open problem of instance optimality in simple graphs. We show that if the problem instance satisfies $n\ge m/16$, where $n$ is the number of nodes and $m$ is the number of edges queried by our algorithm, then it is optimal up to a constant factor. Additionally, we show instance optimality for a broad class of instances, in particular when the largest degree in the graph is at most the square root of the number of explored edges, our algorithm exhibits optimality up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [234] [The Randomized Query Complexity of Finding Minimal Elements in Bounded-Width Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26981)
*Luyao Fan,Jiayang Zou,Jiayang Gao,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在宽度不超过w的n元素偏序集中查找所有最小元素的零错误随机查询复杂度，证明了与已知上界匹配的紧下界，确认了上界的主导常数项是正确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究给出了随机上界，但对应的下界存在一个随w增长而接近2倍的乘法间隙，需要确定上界的主导常数项是否最优。

Method: 基于随机链硬分布下对不可比查询的成对计数，使用分量翻转对合和不可比比较的唯一所有权性质进行论证。

Result: 证明了有限下界公式，并得出对于每个固定的w，查询复杂度为((w+1)/2+o(1))n，确认了已知随机上界具有正确的渐近主导常数。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了上界和下界之间的间隙，证明了对于每个固定宽度，已知的随机上界在渐近意义上是紧的。

Abstract: We study the zero-error randomized query complexity of finding all minimal elements in an unknown $n$-element poset of width at most $w$. Previous work of Daskalakis, Karp, Mossel, Riesenfeld, and Verbin established a randomized upper bound with leading term $\frac{w+1}{2}n$, while the corresponding lower bound left a multiplicative gap in the leading constant that approaches a factor of 2 as $w$ grows. We prove the finite lower bound \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w}\ge \frac{w+1}{2}n-\frac{w(w+3)}4 +w\left(1-\frac1w\right)^n +\frac{w(w-1)}4\left(1-\frac2w\right)^n. \) Consequently, for every fixed $w$, \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w} =
\left(\frac{w+1}{2}+o(1)\right)n. \) Thus the known randomized upper bound has the correct asymptotic leading constant for every fixed width. The argument is based on a pairwise accounting of incomparable queries under a random-chain hard distribution, using a component-flip involution and a unique ownership property for incomparable comparisons. Generative AI was used in the preparation of this manuscript.

</details>


### [235] [Product Structure Meets Track Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27096)
*Michael A. Bekos,Giordano Da Lozzo,Petr Hliněný,Michael Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算法，用于计算具有乘积结构的图的轨道布局，给出了多种图类（如平面图、1-平面图、k-平面图等）的轨道数上界，算法运行时间为线性时间或取决于乘积结构分解的时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定图的轨道数（即图允许轨道布局所需的最少轨道数）是图论中的一个重要问题。本文旨在为具有乘积结构的图提供轨道数的上界，并设计高效算法计算轨道布局。

Method: 提出一种算法，计算强乘积$P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$的每个子图的轨道布局，使用最多$(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$个轨道。结合现有的乘积结构结果，将该算法应用于多种图类。算法仅使用基本的链表数据结构。

Result: 对于平面图，得到的上界与当前最佳上界225匹配；对于1-平面图和最优2-平面图，最多需要375个轨道；对于genus-k、k-平面图、k-framed、k-map和k-string图，轨道数仅依赖于k，为这些图类建立了新的上界。算法对平面图在线性时间内运行，一般情况下时间复杂度为$O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$。

Conclusion: 该算法为多种图类提供了轨道数的新上界，并在计算效率上表现出色，仅使用简单的数据结构即可实现。

Abstract: A track layout of a graph is a partition of its vertices into linearly ordered independent sets, called tracks, such that no two edges between the same pair of tracks cross. Given a graph, the goal in this context is to determine its track number, that is, the minimum number of tracks required for the graph to admit a track layout.
  In this work, we present upper bounds on the track number of graphs admitting a product structure. Our main contribution is an algorithm that computes a track layout with at most $(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$ tracks for every subgraph of the strong product $P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$, where $P^h$ is the $h$-th power of a path $P$, $K_r$ is the complete graph on $r$ vertices, and $H$ is a graph with track number $tn(H)$. Combined with existing product-structure results from the literature, this algorithm yields upper bounds on the track number of several graph classes. For planar graphs, the obtained bound matches the current best-known upper bound of $225$. For $1$-planar and optimal $2$-planar graphs, our algorithm yields track layouts with at most $375$ tracks, while for genus-$k$, $k$-planar, $k$-framed, $k$-map, and $k$-string graphs it provides track layouts with a number of tracks that depends solely on $k$, thus establishing new upper bounds on the track number for these graph classes. The algorithm runs in linear time for planar graphs and, more generally, in $O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$ time whenever a corresponding product-structure decomposition of the input $n$-vertex graph is provided as part of the input, where $t=tn(H)$ and $f_t(H)$ is the time needed to compute a $t$-track layout of $H$. Furthermore, our algorithm only uses elementary linked-list data structures.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Group Isomorphism and the Polylogarithmic-Time Hierarchy: Depth-2$\frac{1}{2}$ Circuits and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26257)
*Joshua A. Grochow,Gülce Kardeş,Michael Levet*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘法表模型下群同构问题的低深度电路复杂性，证明了深度2布尔电路需要拟多项式规模的下界，并给出了深度2.5拟多项式规模电路的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（1970-2025）将电路深度从多项式逐步降至3.5，但均依赖生成元枚举策略且适用于拟群。本文旨在通过利用群特有的结构，突破现有深度限制，探索群同构问题更高效的电路实现。

Method: 采用不同于生成元枚举的新策略：猜测每个群的合成列及其项的生成元和合成因子的同构类型，然后归纳验证两个合成列各层级对应的扩张是否兼容。结合短表示猜想、群扩张算法理论和上同调理论。

Result: 证明了深度2布尔电路判定群同构需要拟多项式规模的下界；构造了深度2.5、拟多项式规模的均匀电路上界。

Conclusion: 通过利用群特有结构（合成列、群扩张、上同调），实现了比先前方法更优的电路深度，为群同构问题的电路复杂性提供了新的理论界限和构造方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the low-depth circuit complexity of Group Isomorphism in the multiplication (Cayley) table model. We prove the first circuit lower bounds for Group Isomorphism: namely, we show that every family of depth-$2$ Boolean circuits deciding Group Isomorphism requires quasipolynomial-size. We complement this with upper bounds of uniform depth-$2\frac{1}{2}$ circuits of quasipolynomial-size.
  A sequence of previous results from 1970-2025 progressively reduced the circuit depth from polynomial to $3\frac{1}{2}$; all of these results relied on the generator-enumerator strategy and, in fact, applied more generally to quasigroups. In contrast, our depth-$2\frac{1}{2}$ construction follows a fundamentally different strategy that exploits structure more specific to groups. We guess a composition series for each group, together with generators for its terms and the isomorphism types of its composition factors. We then inductively verify that the corresponding extensions at each level of the two composition series are compatible. A central part in this approach brings to bear the extensive work on the Short Presentation Conjecture, in tandem with the algorithmic theory of group extensions and cohomology.

</details>


### [237] [Pseudodeterminism and MA != NP^BPP in Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26425)
*Thomas Watson*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 证明了零误差随机通信复杂度与双侧误差伪确定性通信复杂度之间存在指数级分离，改进了Göös等人的工作，并将技术扩展到分离MA和NP^BPP的通信复杂度类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进和简化Göös等人(STOC 2026)关于随机通信复杂度与伪确定性通信复杂度分离的证明，原证明存在双侧误差随机上界的问题。

Method: 通过证明零误差随机与双侧误差伪确定性通信复杂度之间的指数级分离，并推广该技术到通信复杂度类比中的MA和NP^BPP分离。

Result: 实现了零误差随机与双侧误差伪确定性通信复杂度的指数级分离，并成功分离了通信复杂度中的MA和NP^BPP类比。

Conclusion: 该研究在通信复杂度理论中取得了重要进展，提供了更简洁的证明方法，并扩展了分离结果到更复杂的复杂度类。

Abstract: We prove an exponential separation between zero-sided-error randomized and two-sided-error pseudodeterministic communication complexities of partial boolean functions. This qualitatively improves and simplifies the proof of the separation by Göös, Harms, Riazanov, Sofronova, Sokolov, and Yuan (STOC 2026), which had a two-sided-error randomized upper bound. Then, we generalize our technique to separate the communication complexity analogues of MA and NP^BPP.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SLM条件分层关系路由架构，将小型语言模型（SLM）直接集成到图消息选择中，以增强标记属性图的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图神经网络通常将属性表示为静态特征向量，限制了其确定哪些语义证据应影响特定预测目标的消息传播的能力。

Method: 提出SLM条件分层关系路由架构，结合拓扑GNN提供稳定结构表示，使用参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软令牌并生成目标条件路由查询，选择相关消息并在关系级别总结中路由信息。

Result: 该架构提供了有界残差更新到拓扑锚点，保留结构证据同时允许上下文语义信息修改预测，支持在邻居和关系类型级别的可解释分析。

Conclusion: 该架构提供了一种将语言衍生的语义集成到属性丰富的图学习中的通用机制，增强了标记属性图的学习能力和可解释性。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [239] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuronFuzz，一种利用LLM内部安全神经元作为连续反馈的白盒模糊测试框架，通过消除响应生成步骤显著提升了针对强对齐模型的越狱攻击发现效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化安全测试方法主要依赖响应级反馈，需要生成目标模型回复来评估攻击效果，这不仅计算成本高昂，且在面对强对齐模型时，由于大多数候选提示被拒绝且结果单一，导致反馈稀疏，难以提供有效的优化指导。

Method: 提出NeuronFuzz框架：1) 构建SafetyOracle，利用模板无关的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择，识别一组紧凑的安全神经元，将其激活值转化为连续的安全警报分数作为预填充阶段的反馈；2) 利用该分数的可微性，通过梯度识别安全敏感模板位置；3) 结合掩码语言模型生成流畅且上下文兼容的变异，在保留原始有害负载的同时避免引入额外的优化变量。

Result: 在21个文本及多模态模型上进行评估：在5个白盒源模型上，越狱发现率达到76%-100%，优于基线方法最高48个百分点；优化后的模板零样本迁移至开源及6个专有目标模型，平均攻击成功率(ASR)达69.6%，前5集成攻击成功率(EASR)达92.6%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz通过挖掘内部神经元激活信号替代传统的响应生成反馈，成功解决了强对齐模型下反馈稀疏和计算昂贵的问题，实现了高效、高成功率的越狱攻击发现，并展示了优异的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [240] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PyTorch的框架，用于优化二值化神经网络的剪枝，并引入了一种新的全局加权剪枝方法，在保持精度的同时显著提高了剪枝率（VGG11上达70%），优于现有最先进方法（41%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然二值化结合剪枝能进一步提升效率，但现有剪枝策略不适用于二值化表示，难以转化为实际的硬件节省，且缺乏用于设计和优化二值化神经网络的专用研究框架。

Method: 开发了一个集成冻结和剪枝机制的PyTorch框架，并提出了一种考虑跨抽象层学习参数相对重要性的新型全局加权剪枝方法。

Result: 在VGG11模型上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而当前最先进方法在二值化设置下仅能达到41%的剪枝率。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架支持快速评估和原型设计，新的全局加权剪枝机制在模型精度与剪枝率之间取得了更优的平衡，显著提升了二值化神经网络在受限边缘硬件上的部署潜力。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [241] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Muon优化器中使用有限次数的Newton-Schulz迭代（而非精确极分解）对于非光滑非凸优化是有益的，因为它通过平滑奇异值映射确保了收敛性，且所需的迭代深度仅随目标精度对数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要么用精确极因子替代Newton-Schulz迭代，要么将其有限深度视为近似误差，导致无法解释为何实际运行的Muon有效，甚至暗示其可能破坏收敛保证。

Method: 通过“在线到非凸转换”（online-to-nonconvex conversion）框架分析Muon，将更新规则视为在线学习器，并将遗憾界（regret bound）转换为平稳性保证；论证有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极映射平滑为奇异值的Lipschitz映射。

Result: 证明了仅需对数级深度的Newton-Schulz迭代即可保证在非光滑非凸优化中收敛到平稳点，而使用精确极分解更新的Muon可能无法收敛；得出的样本复杂度界限与非光滑非凸优化的最佳已知保证相匹配。

Conclusion: 有限Newton-Schulz迭代提供的平滑效应是收敛的关键，该论证可推广至具有相同平滑性质的其他一般谱映射，为Muon在大模型预训练中的有效性提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [242] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMELS框架，通过结合快速邻域图构建和代数多重网格求解器，解决了传统标签传播算法在大规模高维数据集上计算成本高和内存受限的问题，实现了显著的加速和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习依赖大量标注数据，但人工标注成本高昂且耗时。虽然标签传播（Label Spreading）作为一种半监督学习技术能利用少量标注样本传播信息，但其应用于大规模、高维数据集时，受限于巨大的计算成本和内存约束，难以扩展。

Method: 提出代数多重网格加速高效标签传播（AMELS）框架。该方法通过快速构建邻域图，并引入代数多重网格（Algebraic Multigrid, AMG）求解器替代传统的随机游走迭代。利用AMG的多级特性，AMELS能在单个多重网格循环中将标签信息传播至任意规模的图结构中。

Result: 实验表明，与现有实现相比，AMELS显著减少了运行时间，并且在运行时间和分类准确率方面对超参数选择表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AMELS框架使得在大规模图像数据集上进行高效的标签传播成为可能，即使在仅有少量标注样本的情况下，也能生成准确的标签，有效克服了 scalability 瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [243] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivBoN和PrivITP两种方法，通过在Best-of-N采样中引入校准噪声，同时解决了奖励黑客攻击和人类偏好数据隐私泄露问题，实现了在满足差分隐私的前提下达到最优的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Best-of-N (BoN) 采样策略存在两个主要缺陷：一是容易出现“奖励黑客”（reward hacking），即模型利用代理奖励模型的误差生成投机性回复；二是缺乏对用于训练奖励模型的敏感人类偏好数据的隐私保护。

Method: 1. **PrivBoN**：在奖励分数选择前添加适当尺度的Gumbel噪声，将$\epsilon$-差分隐私与KL正则化对齐相结合。
2. **PrivITP**：针对未知覆盖系数的情况，结合$\chi^2$正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现事后的$(\epsilon, \delta)$-差分隐私，并将正则化参数与隐私参数解耦。

Result: 1. 当隐私预算超过临界阈值$\epsilon^*$时，PrivBoN的隐私噪声即为遗憾最优的正则化，隐私保护不产生额外的对齐成本，达到信息论天际线。
2. PrivITP的隐私成本与响应数量$n$无关，能达到天际线（仅含噪声膨胀项）。
3. 实验表明，PrivBoN和PrivITP具有扩展单调性（解决了BoN在$n$过大时性能下降的问题），且在强隐私机制下，PrivITP的表现优于或等同于PrivBoN。

Conclusion: 通过引入校准噪声这一单一干预措施，可以同时解决推理时对齐中的奖励黑客和隐私保护问题。PrivITP作为一种更通用的方法，在强隐私需求下表现更佳，成功实现了隐私保护与模型对齐性能的最优平衡。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“信息底限”概念，将块草稿（block drafting）中的拒绝损失分解为缺失的块内路径信息和模型差距，发现单个已实现令牌可消除绝大部分信息底限，且当前草稿模型的性能主要受限于模型差距而非信息底限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块草稿机制中，拒绝采样混合了两种损失来源（缺失的块内路径信息和不完美的可观测信息建模），而单纯的接受长度无法区分这两者，导致难以评估草稿模型的真实质量与短程条件的价值。

Method: 提出“信息底限”（information floor）概念，定义为特定条件顺序下的最小预期拒绝率；拒绝率超过该底限的部分被定义为“模型差距”。通过在四个领域、四个开源目标模型和一个前沿API目标模型上进行目标滚动（target rollouts）估算这两个指标，并结合独立互信息分析进行验证。

Result: 1. Qwen3-4B 的最终槽位全并行信息底限为 0.286，限制了单槽位接受率上限为 71%；2. 一个已实现的令牌可消除 86%-100% 的信息底限，证实了局部性效应；3. 当前草稿模型（DFlash, DSpark）远高于信息底限，最终槽位的模型差距分别占其拒绝率的 43%-64% 和 85%-92%。

Conclusion: 研究成功分离了短程条件（short-range conditioning）的价值与提案质量（proposal quality），表明当前草稿模型的主要瓶颈在于模型自身的建模能力差距，而非块内路径信息的缺失。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpEmbed框架，通过分析隐私保护的工单元数据（而非文本内容），为LLM云服务学习紧凑的“操作指纹”，以解决现有基准测试无法反映部署后实际运行行为的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务选型和规划主要依赖能力基准测试，这些测试无法揭示模型部署后的实际运行行为（operational behavior）。在生产系统中，缺乏一种能够利用结构化、隐私保护的元数据来刻画模型运行特征的有效方法。

Method: 提出OpEmbed框架，将模型与时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名。通过时间对比学习（temporal contrastive learning）、跨视图重建（cross-view reconstruction）和代际序数正则化（generational-ordinality regularization）技术，学习低维表示。该方法仅使用结构化工单元数据，不涉及案例文本以保护隐私。

Result: 在Google Cloud上基于26个月内7个LLM家族的33,000多个生产支持案例进行评估。结果显示：OpEmbed能恢复可解释的家族级和版本级结构；在留一模型（leave-one-model-out）操作预测任务中优于非学习基线；在早期窗口数据有限的情况下仍保持有效性；并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed成功构建了LLM云服务的操作指纹，为模型入职（onboarding）、支持就绪评估和运行监控提供了实用工具，填补了能力基准与实际运行表现之间的空白。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [246] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CG4AI框架，通过构建AI模型的凸组合并施加线性约束，确保模型输出满足预定义规则，在MNIST分类和多商品流路由问题上实现了比单模型基线更高的准确性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的机器学习训练仅最小化损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义的规则或约束，而在自动驾驶、网络路由等实际应用中，这种保证至关重要。

Method: 提出CG4AI框架，利用主线性规划（LP）确定最优混合权重，通过定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型以关注最违反的约束，并采用割平面程序将可行性保证扩展至训练集之外。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上实现了四种约束应用（仅从约束学习、提高对抗鲁棒性、纠正误分类、强制输出重标记）；在多商品流问题中成功对神经网络路由预测器强制执行链路容量约束；实验表明该方法能可靠生成可行预测器且准确率优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI框架能有效解决机器学习模型缺乏约束保证的问题，在确保输出可行性的同时提升了模型性能，适用于对约束有严格要求的现实场景。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [247] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜层诊断分类法（Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy）框架，用于构建分类器防护层并识别可信决策，通过优化嵌入维度、定位关键支持向量及构建诊断分类，解决提示注入中的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器在面对提示注入攻击时存在决策脆弱性，缺乏系统方法识别可信决策及处理不同失败模式，需建立端到端诊断框架提升安全性与可靠性。

Method: 1. 构建维度优化分类器，通过交叉验证选择嵌入维度；2. 定位约29%训练样本的潜层支持向量，识别影响标签的关键令牌；3. 基于令牌及攻击幅度构建诊断分类法，划分安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、上下文不足等区域。

Result: 应用于公开提示注入数据集时，发现约77%的自信决策对移除单令牌不鲁棒，失败模式分为置信度校准失败和可利用捷径两类，并为各分类区域提供修复策略。

Conclusion: 该框架有效识别分类器脆弱决策并提供针对性处理指南，通过诊断分类法实现提示注入场景下的安全决策分层管理，提升模型鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [248] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）框架，解决了传统相关聚类算法在扩展性和归纳能力上的不足，实现了在未见过的图实例上的高效聚类，并展示了其在图分类任务中作为可学习池化层的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相关聚类（CC）算法存在严重的扩展性问题，且本质上是直推式的（transductive），即对于任何新的问题实例都必须从头开始执行算法，无法处理未见过的图实例。

Method: 利用图神经网络（GNN）来解决归纳式相关聚类问题，通过在训练过程中学习利用共同的结构模式和节点特征，使框架能够泛化到来自同一分布的新图，且计算开销极小。

Result: 在归纳设置下，推理时间降低了5个数量级，同时保持近似比率在最佳基线解决方案的10%以内；在标准（直推式）CC基准测试中也取得了有竞争力的结果；作为图分类的可学习池化机制，增强了GNN捕捉网络中层次结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅有效解决了相关聚类的扩展性和归纳性问题，还在标准基准测试中表现优异，并可作为高效的池化层应用于图分类任务，提升了GNN的层次结构信息捕捉能力。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [249] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准，旨在解决联邦学习在预测性维护中缺乏统一评估框架的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的预测与健康管理（PHM）是工业4.0的关键，但RUL估计模型的开发常受限于故障数据稀缺。联邦学习虽能协作训练模型而不共享数据，但目前缺乏统一的评估框架。

Method: 引入FedCMAPSS基准，定义五个标准化任务模拟真实工业挑战（从理想IID设置到极端统计异质性），并系统评估多种神经架构下的最先进联邦优化算法。

Result: 建立了可复现的基线，公开了源代码和数据划分，为开发和比较联邦预测性维护解决方案提供了标准基础。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS填补了联邦RUL估计评估框架的空白，推动了联邦学习在工业预测性维护中的标准化研究和应用。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [250] [Bayesian methods and Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms for curve reconstruction and point cloud data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26490)
*Asir Intesar Tushar,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于点云数据表示和闭合曲线重建的全贝叶斯框架，通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器进行后验推断，实现了准确的重建和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代成像和传感器技术捕获的点云数据存在数据量大、定位噪声和缺失信息的问题，且现有的点云重建管道通常只返回单一的最佳拟合结构，缺乏不确定性量化。

Method: 引入全贝叶斯框架，将观测点建模为潜在位置的噪声扰动，这些潜在位置被约束在由非参数先验正则化的底层曲线上。使用针对点云特性定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器进行后验推断。

Result: 数值实验（包括合成示例和真实世界的 LiDAR 数据集）表明，该方法能够实现准确的重建，并对恢复的曲线进行不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架有效解决了点云数据分析中的噪声和缺失信息问题，并提供了不确定性量化，优于传统仅返回单一最佳拟合结构的重建方法。

Abstract: Point-cloud data routinely captured by modern imaging and sensor technologies provide detailed geometric descriptions of objects and environments, but their analysis is hindered by large data volumes, localization noise, and missing information. In addition, existing point-cloud reconstruction pipelines typically return a single best-fit structure without uncertainty quantification. We introduce a fully Bayesian framework for representing point-cloud data and reconstructing closed curves, in which observed points are modeled as noisy perturbations of latent locations constrained to lie on the underlying curve that is regularized by a non-parametric prior. Posterior inference in our framework is carried out using a series of Markov chain Monte Carlo samplers tailored to point-cloud characteristics. Numerical experiments, including synthetic examples and real-world LiDAR datasets, show accurate reconstructions and quantified uncertainty over the recovered curves.

</details>


### [251] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeoTriFuse框架，通过将数据缺失建模为可靠性信号，解决新生儿死亡率预测中的类别不平衡、异质性风险因素、多尺度时间动态和大量缺失数据问题，在F1和AUROC指标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡率预测面临极端类别不平衡、异质性临床风险因素、多尺度时间动态和大量数据缺失的挑战，传统多模态方法将缺失视为预处理问题而非建模信号。

Method: 提出NeoTriFuse可靠性感知多模态融合框架，将缺失建模为显式可靠性信号动态调节模态贡献，整合静态围产期变量、局部 - 全局时间编码器和患者级统计摘要，通过可靠性引导门控机制联合优化死亡率预测和辅助住院时长目标。

Result: F1分数0.6736±0.0216，AUROC 0.9454±0.0056；消融研究表明局部 - 全局时间架构和患者级摘要分支对预测性能贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异质性观察完整性下提升阈值依赖指标。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知多模态融合是在现实临床缺失条件下进行新生儿死亡率预测的实用方法，敏感性分析表明在附近超参数设置下性能稳定。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [252] [Algorithmic Principles For Multiclass Learning Are Hard To Come By: Limits of Regularization and Proper Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26516)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Vatsal Sharan,Alec Sun,Shang-Hua Teng,Chang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文否定了统计学习理论中的两个基本问题：学习能否简化为适当学习，以及适当或不当的多类学习能否由合适的正则化器捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作表明，所有已知的通用多类学习器都依赖于指数级大的一包含结构的复杂方向，而适当学习和正则化等熟悉的算法原则仍不清楚。

Method: 通过构造反例和理论分析，研究学习问题是否可以简化为适当学习，以及正则化器是否能捕获多类学习。

Result: 1. 存在一个可学习的多类问题，不能嵌入任何可适当学习的类中；2. 适当学习可能需要训练误差，且每个可适当学习的类都存在一个适当的学习器在样本大小为 m 时产生 o(m) 误差；3. 正则化不是一般的学习器，存在可适当学习的类不能被任何结构风险最小化学习器学习。

Conclusion: 学习不能简化为适当学习，正则化也不能作为一般的学习器，但提出了 SRM 可学习性的两个充分条件，并通过揭示偏好的可积性来表征 SRM 可表示性。

Abstract: Two of the most fundamental questions in statistical learning theory are the following: which prediction problems are learnable, and how should they be learned? For the former, elegant answers often take the form of combinatorial dimensions. The latter question, however, has proved considerably more elusive: all known general-purpose multiclass learners rely on intricate orientations of exponentially large one-inclusion structures, and familiar algorithmic principles such as proper learning and regularization remain poorly understood. Motivated by prior work, we ask whether learning reduces to proper learning---possibly over a larger hypothesis class---and whether proper or improper multiclass learning can ultimately be captured by suitable regularizers.
  Our primary results answer both questions negatively, resolving three open problems from prior work. First, we exhibit a learnable multiclass problem that cannot be embedded in any properly learnable class, meaning learning cannot be reduced to proper learning by enlarging the hypothesis class. Second, we demonstrate that proper learning can require training error and characterize this phenomenon precisely: every properly learnable class admits a proper learner making $o(m)$ errors on samples of size $m$, but every prescribed sublinear scale $a_m=o(m)$ is necessary for some properly learnable problem. Third, regularization is not a general learner: we exhibit a properly learnable class that cannot be learned by any Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) learner, and a learnable class that cannot be learned by any local regularizer. We complement these impossibility results with a positive theory that gives two sufficient conditions for SRM learnability and characterizes SRM representability through integrability of revealed preferences.

</details>


### [253] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SF-GNN框架，通过识别并过滤由敏感同质性引起的偏差边，在消息传递过程中从源头缓解图神经网络的结构偏差，实现了公平性与准确性的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平感知GNN方法主要在全局层面约束表示或预测分布，未能显式控制聚合过程中偏差信息传播的局部结构路径；即使在排除敏感属性的情况下，图拓扑和消息传递仍会因敏感同质性传播群体相关信号，导致不公平行为。

Method: 提出子图过滤公平图神经网络（SF-GNN），结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器（如枢纽参与和三角闭合）识别易偏差边；在每一步消息传递中引入随机边过滤机制，选择性降低权重或移除这些边；训练中加入带有预热策略的统计奇偶正则化器以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，SF-GNN在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，实现了持续的公平性提升，优于近期的公平感知GNN基线方法，取得了更好的公平性-准确性权衡。

Conclusion: SF-GNN作为一种轻量级且架构无关的框架，能有效从源头缓解结构偏差，通过局部结构路径控制显著改善图神经网络的公平性表现。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [254] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairGIN，一种用于扩展共享单车系统的需求预测公平感知图神经网络，通过模拟扩展训练、注意力知识转移和公平优化，在保持高精度的同时显著减少收入差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决共享单车系统扩展中的冷启动预测难题（新站点缺乏历史数据导致分布不匹配）以及历史数据中编码的结构性不平等问题（低收入地区低骑行量可能反映基础设施匮乏而非需求弱，直接训练会加剧出行差异）。

Method: 提出FairGIN模型，包含三个核心组件：1. 扩展模拟增量训练（Expansion-Simulated Increment Training），在训练中随机模拟网络扩展以缩小冷启动分布差距；2. 基于注意力的知识转移（Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer），结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将数据丰富站点的表征迁移至数据稀疏的新站点；3. 公平感知优化（Fairness-Aware Optimization），引入收入分层正则化和公平校准部署评分以支持更包容的站点选址。

Result: 在纽约市和西雅图的数据集上进行的实验表明，FairGIN在多种扩展场景下均实现了最先进的预测精度，同时大幅降低了基于收入的差异，且未牺牲系统的整体效率。

Conclusion: FairGIN有效解决了动态网络中的冷启动预测与资源分配公平性问题，证明了在提升预测性能的同时，可以通过算法设计缓解结构性不平等，促进低碳城市出行的包容性发展。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks.
